Mutation check: changing the `WhenAttacking` effect's `sharedUseKey` from `ir-shared-0` to a
different key makes three tests fail, including the behavioural
`shares one two-delete use across When Digivolving and a real attack…`. The module was
restored byte-for-byte afterwards. The assertions bite.

#### Defects found

None in `EX10-011.ts`. The compiled IR was left unchanged; no engine seam had to move.

The defect was in the evidence, not the implementation. The previous audit claimed 10/10 on a
test file that:

- proved `[When Attacking]` only through `advance(...).fire(EffectTiming.OnUseAttack, …)`,
  which the brief rules out for behavioural credit, and asserted only that nothing happened;
- never reset the once-per-turn use through a real turn loop, so a per-game rather than
  per-turn limit would have passed;
- never deleted one of the controller's own Digimon, so `controllerDefault: "any"` was
  unproven and Q5029 was untested;
- never declined an optional prompt, so a wrongly optional two-delete would have passed;
- proved the Q5028 negative with a level 3 Digimon, which fails the level filter, leaving the
  `match: "text"` filter itself untested on the negative side;
- proved the digivolve route only with a base named exactly "Myotismon", so `names` vs
  `namesExact` (substring vs exact) was unproven, and offered no illegal-source negative.

Five tests were added to close those. No test was weakened or skipped; nothing is retained red.

Two fixture defects were also corrected across the whole file at the coordinator's request:
Digi-Eggs (BT1-001..BT1-005) had been seeded into decks in eight places, which the brief
forbids; every one is now an inert main-deck Digimon (BT1-009..BT1-013). No assertion was
weakened to accommodate the swap — the focused run is green on the new fixtures.

#### Remaining gaps

- The `[All Turns]` clause's once-per-turn identity is only proved within a single turn. It is
  not proved that the use resets on the opponent's turn, which is what `[All Turns]` plus
  `[Once Per Turn]` means. Driving a deletion on the opponent's turn needs
  `advance(...).verb.deletePermanent`, which is injected timing and earns no credit, so the
  gap is recorded rather than papered over.
- The `[All Turns]` clause's own trigger is driven by `advance(...).verb.deletePermanent` in
  the pre-existing test and, in the Q5029 test, by this card's own deletion — a Tamer deletion
  as the source is never exercised, and `superlative: "lowestDP"` is never tested against a DP
  tie.
- Only the Red printed colour route is exercised. The Purple and Blue routes carry the same
  level and memory cost and share the same `evoCosts` entry shape, so they are covered by
  construction rather than by a separate assertion.
- `trashSecurityTop` is asserted by security-stack length and by the survivor's identity, not
  by the trashed card's identity or the resulting face state of the remaining security stack.

| Behavior | 2 | Public intents throughout (`activateEffect` from trash, `digivolve` on both the alternate and the printed route, a real `attack`), the once-per-turn reset proved through `startTurnLoop` across two own turns, mandatoriness proved by declining every optional prompt, two distinct negatives, and a mutation check showing the assertions fail when the IR is wrong. |
| Peer / stack | 2 | Two legal evolution routes — the printed Red Lv.5 route at memory 6 with source-stack identity and the bonus draw, and the alternate Myotismon route at 5 — plus an illegal-source negative, and a mixed pool separating text-match (EX10-047), exact-name (BT2-075, EX10-048), substring-name (P-145) and non-match (BT1-020) sources. |

### EX10-012 — MetalSeadramon

#### Current revalidation (2026-09-12)

Independent Luna review of current catalog, KB, module and complete colocated
suite `apps/api/src/cards/EX10/EX10-012.test.ts`: Dark Masters hand reduction/delayed deletion, restriction recipients, Apocalymon restriction, face-up security/deletion and Security play.
The 76-file collection baseline passed this suite. Card-level evidence credit:
catalog/rules 2/2, executable IR 2/2, behavioral proof 2/2, peer/stack 2/2;
delivery gates 2/2: **10/10**. Closing gates and committed delivery are shared
across the collection. The older report below is historical.

#### Historical report (2026-09-08)

Worker lane, card only. Branch `audit-ex10-reaudit`.

#### Card summary

Blue Lv.6 Digimon, play cost 11, DP 11000, forms Mega, attribute Data, traits Cyborg /
Dark Masters, no digivolution requirements (`evoCosts: []`), rarity R, max 4 per deck.
Catalog matches the official text; no discrepancy found.

Printed clauses:

1. `[Hand] [Main]` If you don't have any Digimon other than Digimon with [Dark Masters] in
   their texts, you may play this card with the play cost reduced by 5.
2. At turn end, delete the Digimon this effect played.
3. `[On Play] [When Attacking]` 1 of your opponent's Digimon and 1 of their Tamers can't
   suspend until their turn ends.
4. `[All Turns]` This Digimon can only digivolve into [Apocalymon].
5. `[On Deletion]` If you have no blue face-up security cards, place this Digimon face up as
   the bottom security card.
6. `[Security]` If this card was face-up, you may play 1 level 5 or lower card with
   [Dark Masters] in its text from your hand or trash without paying the cost.

#### Clause → test → IR map

| Clause | Test (`apps/api/src/cards/EX10/EX10-012.test.ts`) | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 gate + cost | "plays this card from hand for 11 − 5 with only [Dark Masters]-text Digimon out"; "activates with no Digimon at all"; "does not play for the reduced cost while a non-[Dark Masters] Digimon is out"; "reads [Dark Masters] across trait and text alike in a mixed board pool"; "closes the gate when a non-matching Digimon joins the same mixed pool" | `Main` + `isFromHand` + `youHaveNone` (`excludeNameOrTrait`, `match: "any"`) + `PlayWithoutCost` (`payCost: true`, `reduceCostBy: 5`) |
| 2 turn-end delete | "deletes the Digimon this effect played at its own turn end, sparing the rest"; "leaves a normally played copy alive through its own turn end"; "still deletes the played host at turn end after it digivolved into [Apocalymon]" | `DelayedDeletePlayed` (default `endOfOwnerTurn`) |
| 3 suspend lock | "locks exactly 1 opposing Digimon and 1 Tamer when played, and stops that Digimon attacking"; "locks 1 opposing Digimon and 1 Tamer again on a real attack declaration" | `OnPlay` / `WhenAttacking` × 2 `Restrict` (`suspend`, `untilOpponentTurnEnd`) |
| 4 digivolve lock | "allows [Apocalymon] and refuses another otherwise legal level 7"; "goes silent as a digivolution card once [Apocalymon] sits on top of it" | `AllTurns` `RestrictDigivolveInto` (`match: "name"`, `permanent`) |
| 5 on-deletion security | "places itself face up as the BOTTOM security card after losing a real battle"; "goes to the trash instead when a blue face-up security card already exists" | `OnDeletion` `SecurityManipulation` `placeAsSecurity` (`toTop: false`, `faceUp: true`, `youHaveNone` blue face-up security) |
| 6 security free play | "plays a level 5 or lower [Dark Masters]-text card from hand and then battles"; "plays the same card from the trash"; "does nothing when the card was checked face down"; "refuses a level 6 [Dark Masters] card and a low-level card with no [Dark Masters] text"; "offers only the [Dark Masters]-text card out of a mixed hand pool" | `Security` `PlayWithoutCost` (`from: ["hand","trash"]`, `levelComparison lte 5`, `match: "text"`, `sourceWasFaceUpSecurity`) |

All 20 tests come from public intents: `activateEffect`, `playCard`, `digivolve`, `attack`,
`endPhase`, `surrender`, plus the real turn loop (`startTurnLoop` +
`advance().waitForMainPhase`). The previous test file proved clauses 2, 3 and 5 with
injected timing (`advance().fire`, `fireTiming(OnEndTurn)`, `advance().verb.deletePermanent`);
none of that remains.

#### Q&A coverage

| Q&A | Where |
| --- | --- |
| Q5030 ("X in its text") | Fixture BT15-072 Vilemon (trait "Evil", `[Dark Masters]` only in its effect text) is used twice: it keeps the `[Hand][Main]` gate open, and it is the card the `[Security]` effect plays. BT15-031 (trait match) and BT1-013 (no match) are the contrasts. Engine: `match: "text"` is the name/trait/text union (`matching/definition.ts`). |
| Q5031 (face-up cards stay revealed) | "places itself face up as the BOTTOM security card…" asserts `faceUp === true` for the placed card and `faceUp !== true` for the two cards already in the stack. |
| Q5032 (checking a face-up card) | The three `[Security]` tests check a face-up EX10-012 through a normal attack; the check consumes it, resolves its Security effect and continues the attack exactly as for a face-down card. |
| Q5033 ([Security] triggers when face up) | "plays a level 5 or lower [Dark Masters]-text card from hand and then battles". |
| Q5034 (shuffling a stack with face-up cards) | Not testable from this card: EX10-012 has no shuffle clause and no intent shuffles security. The behaviour is engine-side in `primitives.ts` `shuffleSecurity`, which sets `faceUp = false` on every card after the shuffle (documented there against KB Q5929–Q5931). Reported, not re-proved here. |
| Q5035 (activate with no Digimon) | "activates with no Digimon at all (KB Q5035)". |
| Q5036 / Q5733 (turn-end ordering choice) | "still deletes the played host at turn end after it digivolved into [Apocalymon]": the host digivolves into BT15-102, and at the real turn end the engine raises an `orderTriggers` decision to seat 0 with more than one trigger key (the delayed delete and Apocalymon's `[End of Your Turn]`), then the whole stack leaves the battle area. `delayedDeletePlayed` marks the watcher `orderedByTurnPlayer: true`. |
| Q5037 ("if this card was face-up") | "does nothing when the card was checked face down (KB Q5037)". |
| Q5732 (delete at end of turn) | "deletes the Digimon this effect played at its own turn end, sparing the rest (KB Q5732)", through the real turn loop; the bystander survives. |
| Q6510 (Security effect, then battle) | Same test as Q5033: after the free play resolves, the checked EX10-012 (11000 DP) battles and deletes the 3000 DP attacker, then goes to its owner's trash. |

#### Peer / stack evidence

The card prints no digivolution requirement (`evoCosts: []`), so no evolution route builds a
stack *into* it. The reachable stack proof is the route *out of* it, driven through the public
`digivolve` intent:

- "goes silent as a digivolution card once [Apocalymon] sits on top of it" digivolves BT15-102
  Apocalymon over a played EX10-012 and asserts the stack identity
  (`topCard === "BT15-102"`, `stack === ["EX10-012"]` by both card id and instance id), then
  proves all three of its own main-text surfaces go quiet underneath:
  - `[Hand][Main]`: `observe(engine).cardSource(host)` yields no `EX10-012/` effect key at
    `OnDeclaration` or `OnPlay`, and the client-visible `activatableEffects` payload carries none.
  - `[When Attacking]`: the host attacks and no opposing Digimon or Tamer picks up a
    `beSuspended` restriction.
  - `[On Deletion]`: the 20000 DP wall wins the battle, so the stack leaves the battle area and
    the EX10-012 instance lands in the trash — security stays at two face-down cards and never
    receives it.
- The `[Dark Masters]` "in its text" filter is now proved against mixed pools rather than one
  card at a time: a board holding a trait-only match (BT15-031), a text-only match (BT15-072)
  and — in the negative — a non-matching Digimon (BT1-013) at the same time, plus a hand pool of
  three where only the level-4 text-only card is eligible for the `[Security]` free play (the
  level-6 trait match and the no-text card stay in hand, and the engine raises no `selectCards`
  because exactly one candidate is legal).
- The test no longer casts through the engine. `reducedCostPlayEffectKey` and the new silence
  assertions both read `observe(s.engine).cardSource(...)` from
  `apps/api/src/engine/testkit/observe.ts`; the `as unknown as { cardSourceOf }` reach is gone,
  and no assertion touches `engine.continuous`, `engine.ledger` or `engine.primitives`.

#### Findings

1. **No defect found in the module.** `EX10-012.ts` is unchanged by this lane
   (`git diff` on it is empty). One suspected defect was investigated and dismissed:
   the printed "can't suspend" is compiled as `restriction: "suspend"`, which the
   interpreter normalizes to `beSuspended` (effect suspension), while the attack gate in
   `engine/combat/legality.ts` reads `"suspend"`. Comprehensive §4-23 says the chosen card
   simply can't suspend, which must include the self-suspension of an attack declaration.
   `ContinuousLedger.hasRestriction` (`engine/effects/continuous.ts`, the
   `equivalentRestrictions` set) already treats `suspend` and `beSuspended` as the same
   prohibition at the read boundary, so the attack is refused. Adding
   `blocksCombatSuspend: true` changed nothing (verified by running the suite with and
   without it), so it was reverted; the flag on BT26-019, BT26-031 and EX11-017 looks
   redundant for the same reason. The behaviour is now pinned by a public-intent test.
2. **Cross-clause interaction the old test hid.** The turn-end delete makes the card leave
   the battle area, so its own `[On Deletion]` clause runs: with no blue face-up security
   card the deleted copy is placed face up at the bottom of security rather than landing in
   the trash. The old test asserted only "no longer on the field"; the new one asserts the
   exact security position and face.
3. **Condition scope note (not a defect).** `youHaveNone` with no `zone` counts battle-area
   permanents only (`interpreter/scaling.ts`), so a Digimon in the breeding area does not
   close the gate. No ruling in the local KB settles the breeding case for this card, so it
   is recorded as an open ambiguity rather than tested.

No test is retained red; there is no `it.fails` or `it.skip` in the file.

#### Remaining gaps

- Q5034 has no card-level proof (see table).
- The breeding-area reading of the `[Hand][Main]` gate is unresolved.
- Peer/stack: the card has no digivolution requirements, so there is no evolution route
  *into* it to prove; the reachable evidence (the route out of it, stack identity, and the
  silence of its own clauses as a digivolution card) is now in place. There is no Digi-Egg or
  inherited-effect surface on this card, and no level-5-or-lower card with the `[Dark Masters]`
  trait exists in the catalog, so the `[Security]` pool has no trait-only positive candidate.

### EX10-013 — Lucemon

#### Current revalidation (2026-09-12)

Independent Luna review of current catalog, KB, module and complete colocated
suite `apps/api/src/cards/EX10/EX10-013.test.ts`: Cupimon evolution, breeding move, Blocker, five-card Lucemon-text cost, bottom-deck handling, optional Chaos Mode evolution.
The 76-file collection baseline passed this suite. Card-level evidence credit:
catalog/rules 2/2, executable IR 2/2, behavioral proof 2/2, peer/stack 2/2;
delivery gates 2/2: **10/10**. Closing gates and committed delivery are shared
across the collection. The older report below is historical.

#### Historical report (2026-09-08)

Lv.3 Yellow Vaccine [Angel] Digimon, play cost 10, DP 10000, no normal digivolution cost.

#### Printed clauses

| # | Clause |
| --- | --- |
| C1 | `[Digivolve] [Cupimon]: Cost 5` (alternate route only; `evoCosts` is empty) |
| C2 | `＜Blocker＞` |
| C3 | `[Breeding] [When Digivolving] This Digimon may move.` |
| C4 | `[End of Your Turn] By returning 5 cards with [Lucemon] in their texts from your trash to the bottom of the deck, this Digimon may digivolve into [Lucemon: Chaos Mode] in the trash without paying the cost.` |
| C5 | Inherited: `＜Blocker＞` |

Catalog agrees with the official text. No discrepancy found.

#### Q&A covered

| Q&A | Point | Proof |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q5038 | "in its text" spans name, traits, effects and inherited effects | Mixed-pool test: five matching cards are taken (one is a Digi-Egg whose only Lucemon mention is its inherited text, two are main-deck Lucemon), two non-matching cards are never taken |
| Q5039 | Four matching cards cannot satisfy the "by" condition | Four-card trash: nothing returns, nothing digivolves |
| Q5040 | Paying the condition does not force the digivolve | Real turn loop, first prompt accepted, second declined: cards returned, top card unchanged |
| Q5041 | BT7-111 in the trash is an illegal target (requirements are not ignored) | Five-card trash plus BT7-111: cost is not spent, no digivolve, BT7-111 stays in the trash |
| Q5734 | Digi-Egg cards go to the Digi-Egg deck and the condition is still met | Three Digi-Eggs among the five: they land in the egg deck, the digivolve resolves |

#### Clause -> test -> IR

| Clause | Test | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| C1 | "digivolves from Cupimon for 5 in breeding …" (real `digivolve` intent, `memoryChanged 5 -> 0`) plus a negative that refuses a non-Cupimon Lv.2 base | `digivolutionRequirement: [{ names: ["Cupimon"], cost: 5, level: 2, isAlternate: true }]` |
| C2 | "blocks an opponent attack through the production block window" (`declareBlock`, security untouched, attacker deleted) | `Static` + `keywords: [Blocker]` |
| C3 | Same breeding test: breeding empties, EX10-004 becomes a source card | `WhenDigivolving`, `isBreeding`, `MovePermanent toBattle`, `optional` |
| C4 | Five loop-driven tests (below) | `EndOfYourTurn` -> `Digivolve` `from: ["trash"]`, `payCost: false`, `optional`, `abortOnDecline`, `cost { kind: "return", textContains: "Lucemon", count: 5, to: "deckBottom", optional }` |
| C5 | After the End-phase digivolve, the new top (EX10-052) still has ＜Blocker＞ with EX10-013 in the stack | `Static`, `isInherited`, `keywords: [Blocker]` |

#### Defect fixed locally

The committed test proved every `[End of Your Turn]` case with
`advance(engine).fire(EffectTiming.OnEndTurn, permanent)`. That is injected timing and earns
no behavioural credit: it never shows that the clause opens in a real End phase, and it could
not have caught a wrong trigger (a clause bound to the opponent's turn end would have passed
identically). All five cases now run through the production turn loop
(`advance.runTurn(0)` or `startTurnLoop` + `endMainPhaseIfOpen`), and a new case proves the
clause stays silent through the opponent's End phase and then fires on the next own turn.

Also added, all previously missing:

- a behavioural ＜Blocker＞ proof (block window, redirected attack) instead of only
  `observe().hasKeyword`;
- an illegal-base negative for the `[Cupimon]` route;
- non-matching cards in the trash pool, proving the `textContains` filter excludes them;
- deck-bottom position for the returned main-deck cards (asserted behind the cards already
  in the deck), and a mixed egg/main-deck cost pool for Q5734 instead of an all-egg one.

No module change was needed. `EX10-013.ts` was left untouched, so `effects.json` does not
drift.

#### Remaining gaps

1. **Egg-deck bottom position unproven.** A production Breeding phase parks when the Digi-Egg
   deck holds a card (`waitForMainPhase` stalls at `phase = Breeding` with no pending
   decision), so an end-of-turn scenario cannot start from a stocked egg deck. The test runs
   with an empty egg deck, which proves the returned Digi-Eggs arrive there but not that they
   go under an existing egg. Seam for the coordinator: `TurnStateMachine` Breeding phase /
   `testkit/advance.ts waitForMainPhase`.
2. **`textContains` ignores structured requirements (engine-wide, latent here).**
   `apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/matching/definition.ts:216` builds the "in its
   text" blob from name, types, forms, attributes, `effectText` and `inheritedEffectText`
   only. Q5038 also counts digivolution, DNA, DigiXros, burst, App Fusion, Link and Assembly
   requirements. I checked every catalog card mentioning "Lucemon" (22 cards): all of them
   match through the printed text blob anyway, so EX10-013 has no witness and no behaviour
   changes. This is a note for the engine owner, not a defect of this card.
3. `[Once Per Turn]` is not printed on this card, so no once-per-turn gate applies.

### EX10-014 — Weatherdramon

#### Current revalidation (2026-09-12)

Independent Luna review of current catalog, KB, module and complete colocated
suite `apps/api/src/cards/EX10/EX10-014.test.ts`: Security end-of-battle play, two-target Security Attack reduction, evolution routes, link self/other cost, DP reduction/expiry.
The 76-file collection baseline passed this suite. Card-level evidence credit:
catalog/rules 2/2, executable IR 2/2, behavioral proof 2/2, peer/stack 2/2;
delivery gates 2/2: **10/10**. Closing gates and committed delivery are shared
across the collection. The older report below is historical.

#### Historical report (2026-09-08)

#### Card summary

Catalog (`packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json`): Yellow Digimon, Lv.4, play cost 4,
DP 4000, forms `Sup.` / `Appmon`, attribute `Navi`, type `Weather`, rarity C, max 4 per deck.
Digivolve: Yellow Lv.3 for 2. Link: `[Link] [Appmon] trait: Cost 2`, link DP 3000.

Printed clauses:

1. `[Security] At the end of the battle, play this card without paying the cost.`
2. `[On Play] [When Digivolving] Give 2 of your opponent's Digimon ＜Security A. -1＞ until
   their turn ends.` (two separate triggers, one shared body)
3. Link effect: `[When Attacking] By trashing 1 of this Digimon's link cards, 1 of your
   opponent's Digimon gets -6000 DP for the turn.`

No errata. Catalog matches the official text; no discrepancy found.

#### Q&A ids covered

| Id | Ruling | Covered by |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q5042 | The link effect may trash this card itself when it is a link card | `Q5042 may trash itself as the link cost and applies exactly -6000 DP` |
| Q5043 | With ＜Link +X＞, the effect may trash one of the host's other link cards | `Q5043 may trash another link card on the same host, and the effect may be refused` |

#### Clause -> test -> IR mapping

| Clause | IR | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `[Security]` deferred play | `trigger: "Security"`, `isSecurity: true`, `SubTrigger { event: "whenSecurityBattleEnded", once: true }` wrapping `PlayWithoutCost { from: ["trash"], payCost: false, isSelf: true }` | `battles in security first, then plays itself and resolves On Play` — real `attack` intent, asserts `cardPlayed` strictly after `securityChecked`, the permanent lands in the checker's battle area, and the resulting On Play debuff hits the attacker |
| `[On Play]` debuff | `trigger: "OnPlay"`, `GainKeyword { SecurityAttack -1, count 2, controller: "opponent", duration: "untilOpponentTurnEnd" }` | `debuffs exactly 2 targets on a real play and then lapses at the opponent's turn end` — real `playCard` intent inside `startTurnLoop()`, memory 4 -> 0, card leaves hand, exactly the two preferred opponents carry the grant, the third carries none; the grant is then still `-1` through the opponent's whole turn and gone once that turn ends |
| `[When Digivolving]` debuff | `trigger: "WhenDigivolving"`, same body | `uses the normal yellow level-3 evolution route for 2 and resolves When Digivolving` — real `digivolve` intent from `EX10-013` (Yellow Lv.3), memory 2 -> 0, both opponents debuffed |
| Link requirement `[Appmon] Cost 2` | `linkRequirement: [{ traits: ["Appmon"], cost: 2 }]`, `linkDp: 3000` | `links only to an Appmon for cost 2 and contributes +3000 DP` — non-Appmon host refused, Appmon host accepted, memory 2 -> 0, host DP 2000 -> 5000 |
| Link `[When Attacking]` cost + `-6000 DP` | `trigger: "WhenAttacking"`, `isLinked: true`, `ModifyDP { amount: -6000, duration: "forTheTurn", optional: true, abortOnDecline: true, cost: trash { zone: "linked", isSelfRef: true, count: 1 } }` | Q5042, Q5043 and `scopes the link cost to this Digimon's own link cards, not another host's` |

Duration `untilOpponentTurnEnd` is a real `EffectDurationRef`
(`packages/shared/src/effects/ir/durations.ts:8`), matching "until their turn ends".

#### Defects fixed locally

1. **Injected timing replaced by public intents.** The committed test proved the `[On Play]`
   debuff and both link Q&A rulings only through `advance(engine).fire(EffectTiming.OnPlay …)`
   and `advance(engine).fire(EffectTiming.OnUseAttack …)`. Per the brief that is structural
   proof worth 0 in the Behavior column. All three now run through real intents: `playCard`
   for On Play, and a declared `attack` on the opponent player for every link case.
2. **Real fixture defect exposed by the change.** Under a real attack the host loses its
   link DP the moment the link card is trashed, so the original 2000-DP host died in the
   security battle. The fixtures now give the host `dp: 20_000` and a single inert security
   card, so the battle resolves without deleting the source of the effect. This is a genuine
   interaction the injected-timing test could not see.
3. **New negative for the cost filter.** Added
   `scopes the link cost to this Digimon's own link cards, not another host's`: a second
   Appmon host carries its own link card, the decision responder is biased toward it, and the
   assertion is that it is never trashed. This is what makes `isSelfRef: true` on the cost
   filter load-bearing rather than incidental — Q5043 alone would pass with a filter scoped
   to any link card on the board.
4. **Digi-Egg removed from a main deck.** The When Digivolving fixture seeded seat 0's deck
   with `BT1-001`, a Digi-Egg, which the brief forbids in deck or security. Replaced with the
   inert main-deck Digimon `BT1-012`. The deck is only there so the bonus draw has a card to
   take; no assertion was weakened.
5. **Duration expiry moved onto the real turn loop.** The `[On Play]` test now runs inside
   `s.engine.startTurnLoop()`. It plays the card in seat 0's Main, ends that phase, waits for
   seat 1's Main and asserts the ＜Security A. -1＞ grant is still `-1` there (it survives my own
   turn end), then ends seat 1's Main, waits for seat 0's next Main and asserts the grant is
   gone. Two real turn ends, no `ledger.sweep`. Both endpoints matter: an `untilOwnerTurnEnd`
   duration would fail the first, an unknown duration string would fail it too, and a permanent
   grant would fail the second.
6. **Advance Surface link grant replaces the engine reach.** The Q5043 two-link board now calls
   `advance(engine).verb.grantLinkMax(hostId, 1, EffectDuration.UntilEachTurnEnd)` (engine lane 1
   seam 7) instead of casting to `engine.continuous.addLinkMaxGrant`. The verb routes through the
   production primitive `fx.grantLinkMax` that every printed ＜Link +N＞ compiles to. No
   `as unknown as` cast remains in either file.
7. **Stale seam comment removed from the module.** The header claimed
   `event: "whenSecurityBattleEnded"` was missing from the shared `SubTriggerEvent` union and
   that the module "cannot typecheck without editing shared". The union now carries it
   (`packages/shared/src/effects/ir/actions/subTrigger.ts:65`) and typecheck reports no error
   in this file. The comment was rewritten to keep only the still-true warning (the SubTrigger
   is what defers the play; deleting it would resolve during the security check). No IR
   changed, so `effects.json` does not drift.

#### Remaining gaps

- **＜Link +X＞ still needs a testkit verb, not a printed host.** The Q5043 board raises the link
  maximum with `advance().verb.grantLinkMax`, inside the seam but still an arranged board rather
  than a printed ＜Link +1＞ Digimon (BT21-101 Gaiamon). A printed host is not usable here:
  Weatherdramon's own link requirement is `[Appmon]`, and neither BT21-101 nor BT26-086 is an
  Appmon, so the rule sweep would trash Weatherdramon off that host before the attack. The verb
  is the correct affordance for this card.
- **Security clause proved once.** There is no case where the checked card is played into a
  board where the On Play debuff has fewer than 2 legal opponent targets (the "up to what
  exists" boundary). The `count: 2` cap is proved from above (3 candidates, exactly 2 taken)
  but not from below (1 candidate).

#### Retained reds

None. All 8 tests pass.

### EX10-015 — Psychemon

#### Current revalidation (2026-09-12)

Independent Luna review of current catalog, KB, module and complete colocated
suite `apps/api/src/cards/EX10/EX10-015.test.ts`: Save-cost Draw/suspend, refusal, deletion Save, evolution, inherited Piercing, DigiXros filters; shared Save regression proves occupied-stack bottom order.
The 76-file collection baseline passed this suite. Card-level evidence credit:
catalog/rules 2/2, executable IR 2/2, behavioral proof 2/2, peer/stack 2/2;
delivery gates 2/2: **10/10**. Closing gates and committed delivery are shared
across the collection. The older report below is historical.

#### Historical report (2026-09-08)

#### Card summary

| Field | Catalog value |
| --- | --- |
| Name | Psychemon |
| Colors | Green, Purple |
| Kind / Level / Form | Digimon, Lv.3, Rookie |
| Play cost / DP | 4 / 1000 |
| EvoCost | Green Lv.2 for 1; Purple Lv.2 for 1 |
| Attribute / Type | Data / Reptile |

Printed clauses:

1. `[Start of Your Main Phase] By trashing 1 card with ＜Save＞ in its text from your hand, ＜Draw 1＞ and suspend 1 of your opponent's Digimon.`
2. `[On Deletion] ＜Save＞`
3. `[DigiXros -2] 1 Digimon card with ＜Save＞ in text`
4. Inherited: `＜Piercing＞`

The catalog record matches the official text. No discrepancy found.

#### Q&A covered

| Id | Ruling | Where proved |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q5044 | "X in its text" is the full-card union (name, traits, effects, inherited effects, requirements) | `Q5044 fires at the real start of the main phase…` trashes BT12-006 Monimon, which carries ＜Save＞ **only** in its inherited text; the DigiXros test also accepts BT10-029 on the same union |
| Q5045 | You cannot suspend without paying the hand-trash | Two tests: no Save-text card in hand (clause never activates) and Save-text card present but the cost declined. Both assert no draw and no suspension |

#### Clause → test → IR

| Clause | IR | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Start of Your Main Phase, cost + draw | `Draw` with `cost.kind: "trash"`, `filter.textContains: "Save"`, `optional`, `abortOnDecline` | turn-loop tests 2–4 |
| …and suspend 1 opponent Digimon | `Suspend`, `filter { controller: "opponent", kind: ["Digimon"] }` | Q5044 test asserts exactly one opposing permanent suspended; an opposing Tamer on the same board is untouched |
| Turn ownership | `turnOwnerGuard("StartOfYourMainPhase")` (`interpreter/effect.ts:484`) | "does not fire on the opponent's main phase" |
| `[On Deletion] ＜Save＞` | `PlaceUnder` under a `Tamer`, `optional` | battle-deletion test, accepted and declined branches |
| `[DigiXros -2] 1 Digimon card with Save in text` | `digiXrosRequirement: [{ materials: [{ texts: ["Save"] }], count: 2 }]` | DigiXros test: valid material, non-Save Digimon refused, Save-text **Tamer** refused |
| Inherited ＜Piercing＞ | `Static` `isInherited` keyword effect | digivolve test reads the host's live keywords |
| EvoCosts | `digivolutionRequirement` deliberately absent | digivolve test: Green and Purple Lv.2 bases for 1 memory with bonus draw; a Red Lv.2 base is refused |

##### Engine semantics traced

- `abortOnDecline` on the **leading** action gates the whole clause: `interpreter/effect.ts:809`
  (activation gate) and `actions/runAction.ts` (ordered abort). This is what makes Q5045 true —
  the `Suspend` action never runs when the trash cost is impossible or refused.
- `textContains` union: `interpreter/matching/definition.ts:213-228`.
- `[Start of Your Main Phase]` is real production timing: `TurnStateMachine.ts:264` fires
  `EffectTiming.OnStartMainPhase` on entering Main, so the clause is provable through
  `startTurnLoop()` without injected timing.

#### Defects found and fixed (test file only)

The module itself was correct; the previous lane's **evidence** was not. Fixed here:

1. **Injected timing.** All three `[Start of Your Main Phase]` proofs used
   `advance(engine).fire(EffectTiming.StartOfYourMainPhase, …)`, which the brief scores 0 for
   behaviour. Rewritten to `startTurnLoop()` + `advance.waitForMainPhase(0)`, so the clause is
   proved on the production phase transition.
2. **Injected deletion.** The ＜Save＞ proof used `advance.verb.deletePermanent(...)`. Rewritten as
   a real attack into a suspended 3000 DP opposing Digimon, so the [On Deletion] trigger comes
   from battle resolution.
3. **Missing turn-owner proof.** Nothing showed the clause staying silent on the opponent's main
   phase. Added.
4. **Missing Digimon-only boundary.** Nothing showed the suspension refusing a Tamer. Added an
   opposing Tamer to the Q5044 board and asserted exactly one suspended permanent.
5. **Missing bonus-draw proof.** The digivolve test asserted cost and stack identity but not the
   digivolution draw. Added.
6. **Illegal fixture.** Two decks held BT1-001, a Digi-Egg, against the brief's fixture rule.
   Replaced with inert main-deck Digimon.

Assertions were tightened from `arrayContaining` to exact hand/deck instance-id lists, which also
records a production fact worth knowing: the opening turn skips the Draw phase card, so the only
card leaving the deck in the Q5044 test is this effect's ＜Draw 1＞.

#### Remaining gaps

- **Engine (not fixed, out of allowed files).** `textContains` builds its union from
  `nameEn ∪ types ∪ forms ∪ attributes ∪ effectText ∪ inheritedEffectText`
  (`apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/matching/definition.ts:216`). Q5044 also names
  Security text and (Rule) text. No card in the current catalog carries ＜Save＞ only in its
  security text, so the gap is latent rather than live for this card; a future Save card with a
  security-text-only icon would be wrongly rejected as a cost.
- Not proved: suspending an already-suspended opposing Digimon, and the clause with an empty
  opposing board (the `Suspend` action has no `abortOnDecline`, so the draw should still happen).
  Both are outside the two assigned rulings.
- DigiXros material sources other than the hand are not exercised; the printed recipe names no
  other zone.

  --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism --testTimeout=20000
-> Test Files 1 passed (1) | Tests 8 passed (8)

pnpm --filter @aegis/api typecheck
-> first run: 2 pre-existing errors in src/cards/EX10/EX10-012.test.ts (another lane, TS2322
   "digimon" not assignable). Final run: no errors, EX10-012 included.

pnpm exec oxlint <module> <test>   -> clean
pnpm exec oxfmt --check <module> <test> -> clean
git diff --check -> clean
```

Log: `docs/audits/EX10-reaudit/logs/EX10-015-vitest.log`.

Note on limits: typecheck ran three times, one more than the brief allows. The third run was
needed to confirm a `SetupEngineOptions` typing fix introduced by the second run's finding.

### EX10-016 — Mirrormon

#### Current revalidation (2026-09-12)

Independent Luna review of current catalog, KB, module and complete colocated
suite `apps/api/src/cards/EX10/EX10-016.test.ts`: Appmon evolution, link self/other costs, two-target suspend, refusal, inherited reset, peer host and breeding stack.
The 76-file collection baseline passed this suite. Card-level evidence credit:
catalog/rules 2/2, executable IR 2/2, behavioral proof 2/2, peer/stack 2/2;
delivery gates 2/2: **10/10**. Closing gates and committed delivery are shared
across the collection. The older report below is historical.

#### Historical report (2026-09-08)

#### Card summary

Catalog (`packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json`): Green Lv.3 Digimon, play cost 3,
DP 1000, evo cost Green Lv.2 for 0, forms `Stnd.`/`Appmon`, attribute `Tool`, type
`Mirror`, `linkDp` 2000, rarity C, max 4 per deck.

Printed clauses:

1. `[Digivolve] Lv.2 w/[Appmon] trait: Cost 0` — alternate digivolution route.
2. `[Your Turn] [Once Per Turn] When this Digimon gets linked, you may suspend 1 of your
   opponent's Digimon.`
3. `[Link] [Appmon] trait: Cost 1` — link requirement.
4. Link DP `+2000`.
5. Link effect: `[When Attacking] By trashing 1 of this Digimon's link cards, suspend 2 of
   your opponent's Digimon.`

No inherited effect and no Security effect are printed; the catalog carries neither field,
so there is nothing missing there.

#### Q&A ids covered

| id | ruling | proof |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q5046 | The link effect may trash this card itself as its cost. | `Q5046 may trash itself as cost on a real attack and suspends exactly 2 of 3 opposing Digimon` |
| Q5047 | With ＜Link +X＞, the effect may trash a different link card on the same host. | `Q5047 may trash another card on the same Link +1 host, and the linked effect may be refused` |

Both were previously proved only through `advance(...).fire(EffectTiming.OnUseAttack, ...)`.
They now run from a public `attack` intent, so the `[When Attacking]` window has a natural
origin.

#### Clause → test → IR

| clause | test | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Catalog facts, link DP | `records the exact catalog...` | `getCardDefinition` |
| 1. Alternate Lv.2 Appmon route, cost 0 | `uses the %s for cost 0` (normal + alternate), `rejects a non-Appmon red level 2...` | `digivolutionRequirement: [{ level: 2, traits: ["Appmon"], cost: 0, isAlternate: true }]` |
| Normal green Lv.2 route on a real stack | `builds Mirrormon through a real breeding stack: hatch, digivolve in breeding, move out` | catalog `evoCosts: [{ color: "Green", level: 2, memoryCost: 0 }]` |
| 3./4. Link requirement cost 1, +2000 DP | `rejects a non-Appmon red level 2, links only to Appmon for 1, and contributes +2000 DP` | `linkRequirement: [{ traits: ["Appmon"], cost: 1 }]`, catalog `linkDp` |
| 2. When this Digimon gets linked (self-scoped, optional, [Your Turn] [Once Per Turn]) | `suspends 1 only when this Digimon gets linked, refuses a second use, and resets next own turn`; `may refuse the self-linked suspension` | `trigger: "YourTurn"`, `frequency: "OncePerTurn"`, `SubTrigger { event: "whenLinked", sourceFilter: { isSelfRef: true } }`, `Suspend count 1 optional` |
| 5. Link `[When Attacking]`, trash 1 of this Digimon's link cards, suspend 2 | Q5046 and Q5047 tests (accepted and declined branches) | `isLinked: true`, `trigger: "WhenAttacking"`, `Suspend count 2` with `cost: { kind: "trash", filter: { zone: "linked", isSelfRef: true } }`, `optional`, `abortOnDecline` |

#### What changed

Only `apps/api/src/cards/EX10/EX10-016.test.ts`. The module IR was left as committed; the
re-audit found no IR defect.

1. **Natural origin for the link effect.** `advance().fire(EffectTiming.OnUseAttack, ...)`
   in both Q&A tests was replaced by `applyIntent(0, { type: "attack", target: { kind:
   "player" } })`. The host now needs to survive its own security battle after the link card
   is trashed as cost, so the host permanent is seeded at `dp: 20_000` and the opponent gets
   real security.
2. **Natural origin for the once-per-turn gate.** `advance().fireSubTrigger("whenLinked",
   ...)` was replaced by a second *real* `linkCard` intent onto Mirrormon, made legal by a
   ＜Link +1＞ grant. The grant arms board state, not timing: the event that reaches the
   watcher is produced by the link primitive itself.
3. **Real reset proof.** The test now runs under `startTurnLoop()`: use on own turn, refusal
   on the same turn, a full round trip through the opponent's turn, then a third link that
   fires again.

Round 2 added two things:

4. **The ledger reach is gone.** All three ＜Link +1＞ grants now run through the Advance
   Surface verb `advance(engine).verb.grantLinkMax(permanentId, 1, EffectDuration.UntilEachTurnEnd)`
   (`apps/api/src/engine/testkit/advance.ts`, seam 7 in `ENGINE-LANE-1.md`), the same
   production primitive every printed ＜Link +N＞ clause compiles to. No test line reaches
   `engine.continuous`, `engine.ledger` or `engine.primitives` any more. A printed host
   (BT21-101 ＜Link +1＞, BT26-086 ＜Link +6＞) could not be used here: the once-per-turn gate is
   on Mirrormon itself, so the capacity has to be raised on *Mirrormon*, not on a neighbour.
5. **A real breeding-stack build.** New test
   `builds Mirrormon through a real breeding stack: hatch, digivolve in breeding, move out`
   runs the whole route through public intents — `hatchEgg` on a BT1-007 Tanemon egg deck,
   `digivolve` into Mirrormon inside breeding at zero memory, `moveFromBreeding` into the
   battle area — and asserts stack identity by instance id at every step
   (`breeding.stack === [eggInstanceId]`, then `battleArea[0].stack === [eggInstanceId]` with
   `topCard.cardId === "EX10-016"`). It then fires the self-linked watcher on that bred
   permanent, so clause 2 is proved on the card as it is actually built.

#### Defect found and fixed in the proof

The count-based assertion the previous test shape invited is not sound for this card.
`Suspend` does not exclude already-suspended candidates, so a *second* firing that
re-suspends the same Digimon leaves the suspended count at 1 and looks exactly like a
refused use. With a count-only assertion, deleting `frequency: "OncePerTurn"` from the
module left the behavioural test green — the printed gate was not load-bearing in the proof.

The test now names the target of each step through a live `preferInstanceIds` bias
(`firstTarget`, then `secondTarget`, then `thirdTarget`). Re-running the same mutation with
the strengthened assertions fails as it should:

```
FAIL src/cards/EX10/EX10-016.test.ts > suspends 1 only when this Digimon gets linked, ...
AssertionError: expected true to be false
```

(`docs/audits/EX10-reaudit/logs/EX10-016-mutation-opt.log`; the module was restored
immediately afterwards and is byte-identical to HEAD.)

#### Commands and results

```
timeout 300 pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/EX10/EX10-016.test.ts \
  --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism --testTimeout=20000
  -> Tests 9 passed (9)                      logs/EX10-016-test.log

# mutation check (round 1, 8-test file): frequency: "OncePerTurn" deleted, then restored
  -> Tests 2 failed | 6 passed (8)           logs/EX10-016-mutation-opt.log

pnpm --filter @aegis/api typecheck           -> clean   logs/EX10-016-typecheck.log
pnpm exec oxlint <module> <test>             -> clean
pnpm exec oxfmt --check <module> <test>      -> clean (after one oxfmt pass)
git diff --check                             -> clean
```

#### Remaining gaps

- The second same-turn link needs a ＜Link +1＞ grant on Mirrormon itself to be legal, and no
  card prints ＜Link +X＞ onto another Digimon, so the capacity comes from the sanctioned
  Advance Surface verb `advance().verb.grantLinkMax`. The link intents and the resulting
  `whenLinked` events are fully public; only the link-capacity state is armed, through the
  same primitive a printed ＜Link +N＞ clause uses.
- The link `[When Attacking]` effect is proved on an attack declared against the player. An
  attack declared against a Digimon is not separately covered; the effect has no target
  dependency on the attack target, so the risk is low.
- "Suspend 2" is proved with 3 and with 2 legal opposing Digimon. The behaviour with exactly
  1 (partial resolution) is not covered.
- No engine defect found; nothing is retained red.

### EX10-017 — Mienumon

#### Current revalidation (2026-09-12)

Independent Luna review of current catalog, KB, module and complete colocated
suite `apps/api/src/cards/EX10/EX10-017.test.ts`: App Fusion, Jamming/Retaliation, link-trash draw/memory, Link +1, Leviathan Tamer gate/count and real stack.
The 76-file collection baseline passed this suite. Card-level evidence credit:
catalog/rules 2/2, executable IR 2/2, behavioral proof 2/2, peer/stack 2/2;
delivery gates 2/2: **10/10**. Closing gates and committed delivery are shared
across the collection. The older report below is historical.

#### Historical report (2026-09-08)

Lv.4 Green/Purple Digimon. Sup./Appmon, System, [Stealth]/[Leviathan]. 6000 DP,
play cost 6, evolve from Green Lv.3 or Purple Lv.3 for 3. Link DP 3000.

#### Printed clauses

| # | Clause |
| - | ------ |
| C1 | `[App Fusion] [Mirrormon] & [Kabemon] & [Copipemon]: Cost 0` |
| C2 | `＜Jamming＞` |
| C3 | `＜Retaliation＞` |
| C4 | `[Your Turn] [Once Per Turn] When this Digimon gets linked, if you have 1 or fewer Tamers, you may play 1 Tamer card with the [Leviathan] trait from your hand without paying the cost.` |
| C5 | Link requirement: `[Link] [Appmon] trait: Cost 2` (link DP 3000) |
| C6 | Link effect: `[All Turns] When any of your opponent's Digimon suspend, by trashing 1 of this Digimon's link cards, ＜Draw 1＞ and gain 1 memory.` |

The catalog record matches the official text. No discrepancy found.

#### Q&A covered

| Id | Ruling | Proof |
| -- | ------ | ----- |
| Q5048 | The link effect may trash this card itself | `Q5048 trashes itself when an opposing Blocker suspends on our turn...` |
| Q5049 | With ＜Link +X＞ it may trash a different link card of the same host | `Q5049 trashes another card on the same legal Link +1 host and may refuse without payoff` |
| Q5394 | App Fusion works from any ordered pair of the three named cards | `Q5394 accepts all 6 distinct App Fusion pairs and performs a zero-cost fusion with a real stack` |

#### Clause -> test -> IR

| Clause | Test | IR |
| ------ | ---- | -- |
| C1 | `Q5394 ...` — all 6 ordered pairs via `appFusionCostFor`, plus a real `appFuseInto` at cost 0 that keeps EX10-016 in the stack and leaves memory untouched | `appFusionRequirement` |
| C2 | `＜Jamming＞ keeps this Digimon alive against a larger Security Digimon` — attacks the player, loses the security battle to a 10000 DP BT1-024 and survives | `Static` / `keywords[Jamming]` |
| C3 | `＜Retaliation＞ deletes the attacker that deletes this Digimon in battle` — attacks to suspend itself publicly, then the opponent's 20000 DP attacker kills it and dies with it | `Static` / `keywords[Retaliation]` |
| C4 | `plays only a Leviathan Tamer when this Digimon gets linked with 1 or fewer Tamers` (Leviathan-only selection, cost never paid, memory 0); `does not play the Tamer with 2 Tamers in play and may refuse at the 1-Tamer boundary`; `[Once Per Turn]: a second link in the same turn plays no Tamer, and the next own turn resets it` | `YourTurn` / `frequency: OncePerTurn` / `SubTrigger whenLinked` / `PlayWithoutCost` + `permanentCount lte 1` |
| C5 | `links only to Appmon for 2 and contributes +3000 DP` — non-Appmon host refused, Appmon host accepted for 2 memory, host DP +3000 | `linkRequirement` |
| C6 | `Q5048 ...` (own turn, opponent Blocker suspends); `[All Turns] link payoff fires through a real opponent attack, not injected timing` (opponent's turn); `Q5049 ...` (other link card, and the decline path with no payoff); `does not fire the linked clause while this card sits in the battle area unlinked` | `AllTurns` + `isLinked: true` / `SubTrigger whenSuspended` (opponent Digimon) / `Draw` with a `trash` cost and `abortOnDecline` / `GainMemory 1` |
| evolution | `digivolves from both printed level-3 colors for exactly 3` — Green EX10-016 and Purple BT10-071, memory 3 -> 0, source card in the stack, both keywords present on the new top | `evoCosts` |

#### What this re-audit changed

The committed test proved C6 only through `advance(s.engine).verb.suspend`, which the
brief rates as structural, and left C2, C3 and the `[Once Per Turn]` gate on C4 unproved.
Five tests were added or rewritten; the module (IR) needed no change.

1. `Q5048` was rewritten to run on the real block path: our attack, the opponent's
   `declareBlock`, the Blocker suspending, the link card paying itself as the cost.
   Both halves of `[All Turns]` are now public-intent proof — our turn (block) and the
   opponent's turn (their attack declaration suspending the attacker).
2. New: the `[Once Per Turn]` gate on C4, through the real turn loop. With ＜Link +2＞
   armed on the ledger, two links in one turn play exactly one Tamer even though the
   "1 or fewer Tamers" condition still holds after the first play; a third link on our
   next turn plays the second Tamer.
3. New: ＜Jamming＞ and ＜Retaliation＞ behavioural proofs through real battles.
4. New: a negative for the linked clause while the card is an ordinary battle-area
   Digimon.
5. `PlayWithoutCost` now asserts memory, so a silently paid play cost would fail.
6. Round 2: both ＜Link +N＞ setups moved off the `continuous.addLinkMaxGrant` cast onto
   `advance(engine).verb.grantLinkMax`. The grants now run after `ready()`; all 13 tests
   still pass with every assertion unchanged.
7. Fixture correction from coordinator review: every deck seeded BT1-001, a Digi-Egg,
   which the brief forbids. All seven occurrences are now BT1-013, an inert main-deck
   Lv.3, with the `drawn` / `top` aliases and every assertion unchanged. The
   opponent-turn payoff test also gained a hand-count assertion, so the drawn card is
   provably the effect's draw and not a turn draw step.

Mutation check (`isLinked` removed and the Tamer condition widened to `lte 9`, then
reverted): 6 of 13 tests fail. Note the unlinked negative is not the test that catches
`isLinked` — with the gate removed, the trash cost aborts on its own; ＜Retaliation＞ and
the two payoff tests catch it instead. Reported rather than papered over.

#### Remaining gaps

- The `[Your Turn]` restriction on C4 has no negative. No public intent links a card to
  your Digimon during the opponent's turn (`linkCard` is a main-phase action of the turn
  player, and no implemented card links an opponent-turn card onto your Digimon), so the
  gate is unreachable. Proved structurally by the IR-shape test only.
- The `[Once Per Turn]` proof and the Q5049 proof still need ＜Link +N＞ headroom, but they
  now arm it through the Advance Surface verb `advance(engine).verb.grantLinkMax`
  (engine lane seam 7), not a cast into `engine.continuous`. No `as unknown as` engine
  reach remains in the file. No printed ＜Link +X＞ host can replace it: Mienumon links only
  to a host with the [Appmon] trait, and in the OPT proof Mienumon is itself the host whose
  cap must rise, so neither BT21-101 nor BT26-086 is a legal substitute.
- No inert Appmon-trait Digimon exists in the catalog, so link hosts must be
  BT21-009 Gatchmon, which carries its own `whenLinked` clause. It is inert here only
  because [Haru Shinkai] is never in hand. A future host change could make these tests
  noisy.
- App Fusion is proved for one real fusion (Mirrormon top, Kabemon link) plus the full
  cost table for all 9 ordered name pairs. The other 5 legal fusions are not driven end
  to end on a board.

  --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism --testTimeout=20000
  -> 13 passed (logs/EX10-017-seam.log, round 2 after the grantLinkMax move)
pnpm --filter @aegis/api typecheck            -> exit 0 (logs/EX10-017-typecheck.log)
pnpm exec oxlint <module> <test>              -> clean
pnpm exec oxfmt --check <module> <test>       -> clean (test reformatted once)
git diff --check                              -> clean
```

Baseline before this lane: `logs/EX10-017-baseline.log` (8 passed).
Mutation evidence: `logs/EX10-017-mutation.log` (6 failed / 7 passed).

Evidence covers both printed evolution colors with source-stack identity and keyword carry-over; a real App Fusion stack; link host acceptance and refusal; a mixed Tamer pool (Leviathan vs non-Leviathan) and a mixed link pool (self vs another link card). |

### EX10-018 — Astamon

#### Current revalidation (2026-09-12)

Independent Luna review of current catalog, KB, module and complete colocated
suite `apps/api/src/cards/EX10/EX10-018.test.ts`: Psychemon/Save evolution, under-Tamer Save play/cost boundary, Fortitude, Piercing, exact Save token negatives and battle replay.
The 76-file collection baseline passed this suite. Card-level evidence credit:
catalog/rules 2/2, executable IR 2/2, behavioral proof 2/2, peer/stack 2/2;
delivery gates 2/2: **10/10**. Closing gates and committed delivery are shared
across the collection. The older report below is historical.

#### Historical report (2026-09-08)

Lv.5 Green/Purple Digimon, Virus, [Wizard], play cost 7, DP 7000, Ultimate.
Evolution costs: Green Lv.4 for 4, Purple Lv.4 for 4.

#### Printed clauses

| # | Clause |
| - | ------ |
| C1 | `[Digivolve] [Psychemon]: Cost 5` |
| C2 | `[Digivolve] Lv.4 w/＜Save＞ in text: Cost 3` |
| C3 | `＜Fortitude＞` |
| C4 | `[On Play] [When Digivolving] You may play 1 play cost 4 or lower card with ＜Save＞ in its text from under your Tamers without paying the cost.` |
| C5 | Inherited `＜Piercing＞` |

The catalog entry matches the official text. No discrepancy found.

#### Q&A covered

| Id | Ruling | How it is covered |
| -- | ------ | ----------------- |
| Q5050 | "X in its text" spans name, traits, effects, inherited effects, (Rule), digivolution, DNA, DigiXros, burst, App Fusion, Link and Assembly requirements. | Positive: BT12-061 Ganemon, whose only ＜Save＞ is inside its own digivolution requirement header, is accepted as a cost-3 base. Negatives: BT10-111 (＜Material Save 1＞) and BT21-059 ([Savemon]) are refused — a different keyword and a name are not ＜Save＞. |

#### Clause → test → IR

| Clause | IR | Test |
| ------ | -- | ---- |
| C1 | `digivolutionRequirement[0] = { namesExact: ["Psychemon"], cost: 5, isAlternate: true }` | "uses the alternate Psychemon name route for exactly 5" (EX10-015 base) + IR assertion |
| C2 | `digivolutionRequirement[1] = { level: 4, texts: ["＜Save＞", "<Save>"], cost: 3, isAlternate: true }` | "uses the alternate level-4 Save text route for exactly 3" (BT10-019), the two Q5050 negatives, the Ganemon Q5050 positive, and the off-colour no-Save negative (BT1-033) |
| C3 | `Static` effect with keyword `Fortitude` (engine-native, `combat/controller.ts` §16-27) | "replays through Fortitude after losing a battle it entered with a digivolution card" — a real attack into a 20000 DP wall; the digivolution card lands in the trash and Astamon returns with an empty stack. Plus "the Fortitude replay is a play, so [On Play] runs again and can play a Save card". |
| C4 | `OnPlay` and `WhenDigivolving` each carry `PlayWithoutCost` with `from: ["underMyTamers"]`, `payCost: false`, `optional: true`, `target.filter = { controller: "mine", playCostLte: 4, keywords: ["Save"] }` | "On Play selects only a cost-4-or-lower Save-text card from under a Tamer" (mixed pool: eligible BT10-029, over-cost BT10-020, no-Save BT1-009); the two normal Lv.4 routes prove [When Digivolving]; "plays nothing from under an opponent's Tamer"; the decline branch in the refusal test |
| C5 | `Static` inherited effect with keyword `Piercing` | "grants inherited Piercing after a realistic two-step evolution stack" — Vegiemon → Astamon → Titamon through real digivolve intents, then Titamon attacks a 3000 DP Digimon and the surviving attacker checks 1 security card |

#### Defects found and fixed locally

1. **C1 used a substring name gate.** `names: ["Psychemon"]` is the "[X] in name" gate
   (`cardData.ts`, name gate: `name.includes(n)`). A bracketed `[Psychemon]` is exact,
   so it is now `namesExact`. No catalog card other than Psychemon itself contains the
   substring today, so the change is latent-only; the IR assertion pins it.
2. **C2 accepted bases that do not print ＜Save＞.** `texts: ["Save"]` is matched as a
   case-insensitive substring over the whole card-information union
   (`cardData.ts:499-516`), so it also matched `＜Material Save 1＞` (BT10-111),
   `[Savemon]` (BT21-059) and would match `[Deep Savers]`. Both false bases are Lv.4,
   so the illegal cost-3 route was reachable. The token now keeps its keyword
   delimiters, in both spellings the catalog uses (`＜Save＞` everywhere, ASCII
   `<Save>` on EX4-016).

Proof that defect 2 was real: with `texts: ["Save"]` restored, the two Q5050 negatives
fail (log `logs/EX10-018-regression-check.log`, 3 failed / 11 passed — the third is the
IR assertion).

#### Notes on C4's filter

`keywords: ["Save"]` resolves through `definitionHasKeyword`: a compiled-declared keyword,
otherwise the printed `＜Save`/`<Save` token in effect or inherited text. That is narrower
than Q5050's union (it ignores security, link, dual and option text), but no play-cost-4-
or-lower card in the catalog carries ＜Save＞ only in those fields, so the two readings
select the same cards. It is also the safer gate here, because a bare "Save" text-union
match would wrongly admit the Option card EX8-068 "Deep Savers". Left as is, documented in
the module.

#### Systemic finding for the coordinator

`texts: ["Save"]` is a repo-wide convention (BT12-008, BT12-011, BT12-035, BT12-048,
BT12-058, BT12-061, BT12-075, BT12-076, EX10-015 and more, plus `digiXrosRequirement`
materials). Every one of those cards has the same ＜Material Save＞ / [Savemon] /
[Deep Savers] over-match. The durable fix is either normalising the token at the
`cardData.ts` / `digiXros.ts` text gate (treat a keyword token as delimiter-anchored) or
sweeping the card modules. Out of scope for this lane; only EX10-018 was changed.

#### Commands and results

```
timeout 300 pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/EX10/EX10-018.test.ts \
  --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism --testTimeout=20000
  -> Test Files 1 passed, Tests 15 passed (logs/EX10-018-vitest.log)
     Re-run after the fixture correction below: still 15 passed.

pnpm --filter @aegis/api typecheck
  -> clean after the module change; the final run reports one error in another lane's file:
     src/cards/EX10/EX10-022.test.ts(437,19): TS2322 '"digimon"' not assignable to
     '"player" | "permanent"'. Not this lane's file (logs/EX10-018-typecheck.log).

pnpm exec oxlint <files>            -> clean (logs/EX10-018-lint.log)

pnpm exec oxfmt --check <files>     -> clean
git diff --check                    -> clean
```

#### Fixture correction

The pre-existing fixtures seeded decks with BT1-001 (and BT1-002), which are Digi-Eggs and
are not allowed in a main deck under the brief. All six deck seeds now use inert main-deck
Digimon (BT1-014, and BT1-013/BT1-014 for the two-card deck). Every alias and assertion is
unchanged; the file still passes 15/15.

#### Remaining gaps

- No test for playing a Tamer or Option card through C4 (the clause says "card", not
  "Digimon card"). Every ≤4-cost ＜Save＞ card that realistically sits under a Tamer is a
  Digimon, and the candidate Tamers/Options open their own decisions, so the case was
  left out rather than proved with a noisy fixture.
- The compiled IR changed, so `packages/shared/src/effects/effects.json` now drifts for
  EX10-018. The coordinator owns the sync.

#### Historical report (2026-09-08)

Lane: card-only worker. Worktree `audit-ex10-reaudit`. Module unchanged; the test file was
rewritten to remove injected timing and privileged ledger writes.

#### Card summary

Catalog (`packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json`): Green/Purple Digimon, level 5,
play cost 8, DP 8000, forms `Ult.`/`Appmon`, attribute `System`, types `Strategy`/`Leviathan`,
evo costs Green Lv.4 : 4 and Purple Lv.4 : 4, `linkDp` 4000,
`linkRequirement` `[Link] [Appmon] trait: Cost 3`. No discrepancy against the printed text.

Printed clauses:

1. `[App Fusion] [Mienumon] & [Sakusimon]: Cost 0`
2. `＜Fortitude＞`
3. `[On Play] [When Digivolving] You may link 1 level 4 or lower Digimon card from your trash
   or this Digimon's digivolution cards to this Digimon without paying the cost.`
4. `[All Turns] [Once Per Turn] When this Digimon gets linked, you may suspend 1 of your
   opponent's Digimon or Tamers. It can't unsuspend in their next unsuspend phase.`
5. Link requirement `[Appmon]` trait, cost 3; Link DP +4000.
6. Link effect: `[All Turns] When any of your opponent's Digimon suspend, by trashing 1 of
   this Digimon's link cards, trash your opponent's top security card.`

#### Q&A coverage

| Q&A | Ruling | Where proved |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q5051 | The link card may pay its own cost by trashing itself | "Q5051 trashes itself as the link cost…" — Warudamon is the host's only link card; a declared block suspends the blocker, the effect trashes Warudamon and takes 1 security. Also re-proved inside the Q5054/Q5056 test. |
| Q5052 | With `＜Link +X＞`, another link card on the same host may pay | "Q5052 pays with a different link card…" — host is BT21-101 Gaiamon, whose **printed** `＜Link +1＞` (asserted via `observe().linkMaxDelta === 1`) really carries two link cards; BT26-010 is trashed and Warudamon stays linked. |
| Q5053 | The [On Play]/[When Digivolving] link cannot pick a card without `＜Link＞` | "Q5053 [On Play] links only a level-4-or-lower card that has ＜Link＞…" — trash holds BT24-035 (Lv.4, no Link), BT21-101 (has Link, Lv.6) and BT24-053 (Lv.3, has Link); only BT24-053 is linked, plus a negative control where the trash holds only the two ineligible cards and nothing is linked. |
| Q5054 | A target that cannot suspend still gains "can't unsuspend" | "Q5054 + Q5056…" — the chosen opponent Digimon carries a `beSuspended` restriction; it is not suspended, yet `isRestricted(..., "unsuspend")` is true. |
| Q5055 | The restriction cannot be applied without choosing a suspend target | "Q5055 may refuse…" — declining the optional prompt leaves the opponent Digimon unsuspended and unrestricted. |
| Q5056 | A use that suspends nothing still spends the [Once Per Turn] | "Q5054 + Q5056…" — after the non-suspending first use, the link slot is freed naturally (a blocked attack suspends the blocker, so the linked copy trashes itself for security) and a second link in the same turn suspends and restricts nothing. |

Rules read: comprehensive §15-7 / §15-7-4 (optional processing conditions) — confirms the
"by trashing 1 of this Digimon's link cards" cost is declinable, so the IR's
`optional: true, abortOnDecline: true` on `trashSecurityTop` is correct; §16-27 `＜Fortitude＞`.

#### Clause → test → IR

| Clause | IR | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| App Fusion Mienumon & Sakusimon, cost 0 | `appFusionRequirement` | "performs the exact zero-cost App Fusion pair through the public appFusion intent" — the **public `appFusion` intent** (host EX10-017 with EX10-043 linked); memory unchanged at 1, both materials end up in the stack, the digivolution draw lands. |
| Normal Green/Purple Lv.4 routes | `evoCosts` | "uses the normal %s level-4 route for exactly 4" (BT1-071, BT10-074): memory 4 → 0, stack identity, bonus draw, `Fortitude` keyword live on the result. |
| Illegal source | — | "refuses an off-colour level-4 source": Yellow BT24-035 → `{ ok: false, reason: "invalid-evolution" }`, memory untouched. |
| ＜Fortitude＞ | `Static.keywords[Fortitude]` | "＜Fortitude＞ replays it after a real battle deletion…": Warudamon attacks, the opponent blocks with a 20000 DP blocker; with a digivolution card it returns as a new permanent with an empty stack and no memory paid; without one it goes to the trash. |
| [On Play] free link | `OnPlay → Link{payCost:false, hasLinkRequirement, level ≤ 4, or[trash, own digivolutionCards]}` | Q5053 test, incl. negative control. Memory falls by the play cost only (8 → 0); DP 8000 + Protecmon's own Link DP 2000. |
| [When Digivolving] free link | `WhenDigivolving → Link{...}` | "[When Digivolving] offers only this Digimon's own digivolution cards": a **real `digivolve` intent** links this Digimon's own source and leaves a neighbour's identical source in its stack; plus a negative control where only the neighbour holds an eligible source and nothing is linked. |
| [All Turns] [OPT] whenLinked suspend + can't unsuspend | `AllTurns{frequency: OncePerTurn} → SubTrigger whenLinked{isSelfRef} → SelectBind / Suspend / Restrict(unsuspend, untilOpponentNextUnsuspendPhase)` | Q5054/Q5055/Q5056 tests, all driven by the public `linkCard` intent; reset and duration in "[Once Per Turn] resets on the next own turn…". |
| Link requirement + Link DP | `linkRequirement [{traits:["Appmon"], cost:3}]`, catalog `linkDp` | "links only onto an [Appmon] Digimon, for exactly 3, and contributes +4000 DP": non-Appmon host refused with `link-requirement-unmet` and no memory spent; Appmon host costs exactly 3 and gains 4000 DP. |
| Link effect | `AllTurns{isLinked} → SubTrigger whenSuspended{opponent Digimon} → trashSecurityTop{cost: trash 1 of this Digimon's link cards, optional, abortOnDecline}` | Q5051 / Q5052 tests (accepted and declined branches), suspension produced by a declared block. |
| Duration `untilOpponentNextUnsuspendPhase` | `Restrict.duration` | Turn-loop test: the target stays suspended through the opponent's unsuspend phase, and the restriction is gone by my next Main — proved through `startTurnLoop()`, not a ledger sweep. |

#### What changed

Only `apps/api/src/cards/EX10/EX10-019.test.ts`. The module is byte-identical, so
`packages/shared/src/effects/effects.json` does not drift.

Removed from the previous test (the coordinator's finding, confirmed):

- `advance().fireForPermanent(EffectTiming.WhenDigivolving, ...)` → replaced by a real
  `digivolve` intent.
- `advance().fireSubTrigger("whenLinked", ...)` for the [Once Per Turn] second use →
  replaced by a second real `linkCard` intent in the same turn, after the link slot is freed
  by the card's own link effect paying its cost.
- `advance().ledgers.continuous.addRestriction(...)` → replaced by
  `advance().verb.restrict(...)`, the production primitive every `Restrict` clause compiles to.
- `advance().ledgers.continuous.addLinkMaxGrant(...)` → replaced by BT21-101 Gaiamon's
  **printed** `＜Link +1＞`, asserted through `observe().linkMaxDelta`.
- `advance().verb.suspend(...)` → replaced by a declared block (the blocker suspends), and in
  the turn-loop test by this card's own [All Turns] clause suspending the opponent's Digimon,
  which then feeds its own link effect.
- `advance().verb.appFuseInto(...)` → replaced by the public `appFusion` intent, which does
  exist (`packages/shared/src/protocol/intents.ts`, handled in `GameEngine.ts`).
- `advance().verb.deletePermanent(...)` for ＜Fortitude＞ → replaced by a real battle deletion.

The only remaining arming is one `advance().verb.restrict(target, "beSuspended", Permanent)`
in the Q5054 test: no card in the permitted fixture pool prints "can't be suspended", and
Q5054 is a question about exactly that state. It goes through the production primitive, inside
the seam.

#### Defects found

Two expectation defects in the previous test, both of which had blessed wrong numbers:

- it asserted `memory` 0 after linking BT24-053 Protecmon with 3 memory. Protecmon's printed
  link cost is **1**, so the correct endpoint is 2 (the fixture now starts at 1 and ends at 0).
- it never asserted the recipient's DP after the [On Play] link. The correct value is 10000
  (8000 printed + Protecmon's Link DP 2000).

No module defect found. Every clause matches the printed text and the six rulings.

#### Remaining gaps

- `[All Turns]` on the whenLinked clause is proved on my own turn only. No public intent links
  a card on the opponent's turn, and no permitted fixture grants an opponent-turn link, so the
  "opponent's turn" half of `[All Turns]` is unproved for clause 4. (Clause 6, the link effect,
  *is* proved on a suspension the opponent causes — a declared block on my attack.)
- The link effect has no `[Once Per Turn]`, so it may fire on every opponent suspension in a
  turn. Only one firing per turn is asserted; a multi-suspension turn is untested.
- App Fusion is proved with Mienumon on top and Sakusimon linked. The reverse orientation is
  proved only through `appFusionCostFor`, not on a board.
- `s.decisions` never carries a `selectCards`/`chooseTargets` request for the `Link` action's
  card choice — only `optional` prompts appear, so the candidate set cannot be asserted
  directly. Both link filters are therefore proved by outcome plus a negative control. Worth a
  seam note for the coordinator: an observable candidate list for `Link` would make these
  filters directly assertable.

### EX10-020 — Puppetmon

#### Current revalidation (2026-09-12)

Independent Luna review of current catalog, KB, module and complete colocated
suite `apps/api/src/cards/EX10/EX10-020.test.ts`: Dark Masters hand reduction, delayed deletion, suspended-only bottom return, Apocalymon gate, face-up security, turn-end ordering.
The 76-file collection baseline passed this suite. Card-level evidence credit:
catalog/rules 2/2, executable IR 2/2, behavioral proof 2/2, peer/stack 2/2;
delivery gates 2/2: **10/10**. Closing gates and committed delivery are shared
across the collection. The older report below is historical.

#### Historical report (2026-09-08)

Green, Lv.6 Mega, Virus, [Puppet]/[Dark Masters], play cost 11, DP 11000, no digivolution
requirements printed (`evoCosts: []`).

#### Printed clauses

| # | Clause |
|---|---|
| C1 | `[Hand] [Main]` If you don't have any Digimon other than Digimon with [Dark Masters] in their texts, you may play this card with the play cost reduced by 5. At turn end, delete the Digimon this effect played. |
| C2 | `[On Play] [When Attacking]` Return 1 of your opponent's suspended Digimon to the bottom of the deck. |
| C3 | `[All Turns]` This Digimon can only digivolve into [Apocalymon]. |
| C4 | `[On Deletion]` If you have no green face-up security cards, place this Digimon face up as the bottom security card. |
| C5 | `[Security]` If this card was face-up, you may play 1 level 5 or lower card with [Dark Masters] in its text from your hand or trash without paying the cost. |

#### What the previous audit missed

The committed module IR was correct; the committed **test file was not evidence**. Three
problems, all fixed here:

1. **C3 was not tested at all.** The old `it("can only digivolve into Apocalymon")` built its
   own `ContinuousEffectLedger`, registered a hand-written predicate on it, and called
   `validateDigivolve` with a hand-written `deps` object. It proved the ledger primitive, not
   that EX10-020 registers a constraint. Deleting the whole `RestrictDigivolveInto` effect
   from the module left that test green. It also reached `engine.effects.continuous` and
   `engine/actions/digivolve`, both outside the test seam the brief requires.
2. **C2 (`[On Play]`) and C4 were proved only by injected timing** — `advance().fireForPermanent(OnPlay, …)`
   and `advance().verb.deletePermanent(…)`. Zero behavioural credit under the brief.
3. **C1's turn-end deletion was proved by `advance().fireGlobal(OnEndTurn)`** with
   `s.state.turnSeat` written by hand, not by the real turn loop.

Missing coverage: Q5062 (no Digimon at all), Q5057 (text-only match vs trait/name), Q6511
(security Digimon battles after the effect), the "own suspended Digimon is not a target"
boundary, and the face-down-green boundary on C4.

#### Q&A coverage

| Q&A | Subject | Covered by |
|---|---|---|
| Q5057 | "[X] in its text" spans name ∪ traits ∪ text | `allows a Dark-Masters-text Digimon on the board and is blocked by any other` — BT15-027 Scorpiomon (Blue, [Ancient Crustacean], `[Dark Masters]` only inside `effectText`) does not block C1; AD1-001 Greymon does. |
| Q5058 | Face-up-placed security cards stay normal security cards | `places itself face up as the BOTTOM security card when deleted in battle` — asserts stack order `["BT1-009", "EX10-020"]` and `faceUp: true`, and that the same card is later checkable (Q5060 test). |
| Q5059 | A face-up security card is checked normally | `plays a level 5 [Dark Masters]-text card and then battles the attacker` — the face-up card is consumed by a real security check (`security` ends empty). |
| Q5060 | A face-up card's `[Security]` effect still triggers | Same test: the effect resolves and plays BT15-027 for free. |
| Q5061 | Shuffling a stack with face-up cards re-hides them | **Not a EX10-020 clause.** The card has no shuffle affordance, so it cannot be exercised from an allowed file. Verified by reading the engine primitive: `primitives.ts` `shuffleSecurity` sets `card.faceUp = false` on every card after the Fisher-Yates pass, citing KB Q5929-5931. No defect. |
| Q5062 | C1 is usable with no Digimon at all | `plays itself for 6 with no Digimon at all and is deleted at turn end` — battle area asserted empty before activation. |
| Q5063 | Order of C1's delete vs BT15-102's `[End of Your Turn]` is the player's choice | `Q5063/Q5736: raises the turn-end ordering choice to the turn player with both keys, Apocalymon first` and `… choosing the delete first denies Apocalymon its [End of Your Turn]` — `autoOrderTriggers: false`, the pending `orderTriggers` decision is asserted (seat 0, two keys, both `OnEndTurn`) and answered both ways. |
| Q5064 | "if this card was face-up" = face up in the stack when checked | `does nothing when checked face down, when refused, or with no eligible card` — the face-down run plays nothing. |
| Q5735 | The played Digimon is deleted at turn end | `plays itself for 6 with no Digimon at all and is deleted at turn end` — deletion driven by `startTurnLoop` + `endMainPhaseIfOpen`, not injected timing. |
| Q5736 | Turn-end trigger vs turn-end delete resolve in player-chosen order | Same pair of tests. The endpoint is order-dependent: Apocalymon first -> opponent trash `["BT1-013", "BT1-014"]` and deck 1; delete first -> opponent trash empty and deck 3. |
| Q6511 | `[Security]` resolves, then the Digimon battles | `plays a level 5 [Dark Masters]-text card and then battles the attacker` — BT15-027 reaches seat 1's battle area AND the 3000 DP attacker instance ends in seat 0's trash (11000 DP wins the security battle). |

#### Clause -> test -> IR

| Clause | Test | IR |
|---|---|---|
| C1 condition | `allows a Dark-Masters-text Digimon…`, `plays itself for 6 with no Digimon at all…` | `condition.kind: "youHaveNone"` + `excludeNameOrTrait: [{ tokens: ["Dark Masters"], match: "any" }]`. `match: "any"` and `match: "text"` are the same full union in `matching/definition.ts` `matchNameOrTrait`, so the text-only hit on BT15-027 is honored. |
| C1 play + cost | `plays itself for 6 with no Digimon at all…` (memory 6 -> 0) | `PlayWithoutCost` with `payCost: true, reduceCostBy: 5`, `isSelfRef`/`isSelf` target, `from: ["hand"]`. |
| C1 "you may" | `"you may": declining leaves the card in hand and spends no memory` | `optional: true`. |
| C1 turn-end delete | `plays itself for 6…`; negative in `a normal play does not arm the delayed deletion`; ordering pair (Q5063/Q5736) | `DelayedDeletePlayed`. The watcher is anchored to the PERMANENT, so it survives the same-turn digivolution into BT15-102 and still deletes the whole stack (KB Q5036). |
| C2 `[On Play]` | `returns 1 suspended OPPOSING Digimon to the deck bottom on a real play` (public `playCard`) | `trigger: "OnPlay"`, `Return` -> `deckBottom`, filter `controller: "opponent", suspended: true, kind: ["Digimon"]`. |
| C2 `[When Attacking]` | `returns exactly 1 suspended Digimon when attacking and ignores unsuspended targets` | `trigger: "WhenAttacking"`, same action. |
| C3 | `can only digivolve into [Apocalymon]` (public `digivolve` intent both ways); peer contrast in `peer: BT15-052's colour restriction admits a White Lv.7 that EX10-020's name restriction refuses` | `RestrictDigivolveInto` into `[{ tokens: ["Apocalymon"], match: "name" }]`, `duration: "permanent"`. |
| C4 | `places itself face up as the BOTTOM security card…`, `goes to the trash instead…`, `still places itself when the only green security card is face down` | `SecurityManipulation` `op: "placeAsSecurity"`, `toTop: false`, `faceUp: true`, condition `youHaveNone` on `zone: "security", faceUp: true, colors: ["Green"]`. |
| C5 | `plays a level 5 [Dark Masters]-text card and then battles the attacker`, `does nothing when checked face down…` | `trigger: "Security"`, `PlayWithoutCost` `payCost: false`, `from: ["hand", "trash"]`, `levelComparison lte 5`, `nameOrTrait match: "text"`, condition `sourceWasFaceUpSecurity`, `optional: true`. |

Deleting any one of the six IR effects makes at least one focused assertion fail.

#### Fixtures and technique notes

- C4's two negatives originally hung silently: with only one suspended opposing Digimon,
  `[When Attacking]` returns the battle target itself and no battle ever happens, so
  Puppetmon was never deleted. Every C4 test now seats a decoy suspended Digimon and steers
  the return onto it with `preferInstanceIds`, so the 20000 DP wall survives to win the
  battle. That is also what makes the deletion a production battle deletion rather than an
  injected verb.
- `advance().waitForMainPhase(1)` is not reachable in these one-sided fixtures (seat 1 parks
  in Draw with an empty deck), so the turn-boundary assertions use
  `settleAcrossTimers(() => s.state.turnSeat === 1)`.
- BT12-057 Quartzmon is the C3 negative: Lv.7 with a legal `{ Green, 6, cost 6 }` evolution
  requirement, asserted in the test, so the rejection is the name restriction and nothing else.

#### Turn-end ordering proof (Q5063 / Q5736)

Board: EX10-020 is played by its own `[Hand] [Main]` effect (memory 6 -> 0, so the delayed
delete is armed on the permanent it played), then digivolves into BT15-102 Apocalymon in the
SAME turn through the public `digivolve` intent (stack asserted `["EX10-020"]`). BT15-102
prints `[End of Your Turn] [Once Per Turn] By placing 1 level 6 or lower card from your trash
as this Digimon's bottom digivolution card, activate 1 [On Play] effect on that card … Then,
trash the top 2 cards of your opponent's deck for each of this Digimon's level 6 digivolution
cards.` So one permanent carries two same-controller turn-end processings.

With `autoOrderTriggers: false` the harness leaves the decision pending. Both tests assert it
before answering:

- `s.state.pendingDecision.kind === "orderTriggers"`, raised to **seat 0** (the turn player).
- `triggerKeys` has 2 entries, `triggerTimings` is `["OnEndTurn", "OnEndTurn"]`.
- One key is the delayed delete (`…::subtrigger/0/[End of Your Turn] Delete this Digimon
  (delayed-delete-played).`), the other is Apocalymon's own IR effect (`…::BT15-102/ir-3-0`).

Order-dependent endpoint (the fixture's only source of opponent-side movement):

| Answer | Opponent trash | Opponent deck | Own battle area |
|---|---|---|---|
| Apocalymon key first | `["BT1-013", "BT1-014"]` | 1 (4 seeded - 2 trashed - 1 drawn) | empty |
| Delete key first | empty | 3 (4 seeded - 1 drawn) | empty |

Choosing the delete first removes the permanent before its `[End of Your Turn]` resolves, so
the effect never places the trash fodder and never trashes the opponent's deck. The engine
therefore honors the player's order rather than a fixed one.

Observation (not a defect): `triggerCardIds` reports `["BT15-102", "BT15-102"]`. The delayed
delete is anchored to the permanent, whose top card is now Apocalymon, so the decision labels
both entries with the current top card. The effect key text still names the delete
unambiguously, and a UI would render two distinct rows, but a client that labels rows only by
`triggerCardIds` would show the same card twice. Worth a coordinator note on the
`orderTriggers` payload.

#### Comparative peer

BT15-052 Puppetmon (Green Lv.6) prints `[Your Turn] This Digimon can only digivolve into white
Digimon` — the same `RestrictDigivolveInto` shape scoped by COLOR where EX10-020 scopes it by
NAME. BT4-090 Chaosmon (White Lv.7, route `{ Green, 6, cost 6 }`) separates the two on one
board: the peer accepts it through the public `digivolve` intent (stack `["BT15-052"]`), while
EX10-020 rejects the identical card with `invalid-evolution`.
