#### Catalog discrepancy (report only, not edited)

`cards.json` stores a NO-BREAK SPACE (U+00A0) instead of a normal space after the bracket in
both texts:

- `effectText` offset 82: `…with [Dark Masters] in their texts…`
- `securityEffectText` offset 93: `…with [Dark Masters] in its text…`

Harmless for this card (no filter reads the raw string here), but a `textContains`/`match:"text"`
filter on another card that searches for the literal `"[Dark Masters] in"` would miss it. The
catalog assertion in the test carries the U+00A0 so the discrepancy is pinned, not hidden.

The test file did not `import "../index.js";`, so it ran against a card registry holding only

were weaker than they read. Added `import "../index.js";` above the module import. No fixture
needed swapping — all assertions hold with the real registry loaded, including the BT15-102
digivolution stack and the BT15-027 / AD1-001 peer discrimination, which makes those proofs
genuine rather than vacuous. Scores are unchanged (the proofs are the same proofs, now
actually exercised).

The 76-file collection baseline passed this suite. Card-level evidence credit:
catalog/rules 2/2, executable IR 2/2, behavioral proof 2/2, peer/stack 2/2;
delivery gates 2/2: **10/10**. Closing gates and committed delivery are shared
across the collection. The older report below is historical.

#### Historical report (2026-09-08)

Lane: card-only. Worktree: `audit-ex10-reaudit`. Files touched: `apps/api/src/cards/EX10/EX10-021.ts`,
`apps/api/src/cards/EX10/EX10-021.test.ts`, this report, `docs/audits/EX10-reaudit/logs/EX10-021-*.log`.

#### Card summary

Catalog (`packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json`): Lv.6 Green/Purple Mega, Virus,
[Demon Lord]/[Seven Great Demon Lords], play cost 11, DP 11000, evo cost Purple Lv.5 for 3,
rarity C, max 4. No inherited text, no security text. The catalog text matches the official
list; no discrepancy found.

Printed clauses:

1. `[Digivolve] [Belphemon: Rage Mode]: Cost 1` — alternate digivolution route.
2. `[On Play] [When Digivolving] By placing 1 [Belphemon: Rage Mode] from your trash as this
   Digimon's top digivolution card, until your opponent's turn ends, this Digimon can't attack
   and your opponent's effects don't affect it.`
3. `[Opponent's Turn] [Once Per Turn] When any of your opponent's Digimon suspend, by trashing
   2 cards in your hand, suspend 2 of their Digimon or Tamers.`

#### Q&A ids covered

| Q5066 | "Effects don't affect" blocks the processing | Behavioural: "your opponent's effects don't affect it" — the opponent plays BT1-070 Kuwagamon ("[On Play] Suspend 1 of your opponent's Digimon") on their own turn with Sleep Mode as the only Digimon; it stays unsuspended. |
| Q5067 | An unaffectable card can still be **chosen** | **Retained red** (`it.fails`). The engine removes unaffectable permanents from opponent-effect candidate lists, so the choice is never offered. Seam named below. The end state is still rules-correct. |
| Q5068 | It can be given an effect, but is not considered to have it | Not behaviourally reachable from this card: it needs a second card that grants a keyword to an opponent's Digimon. Implemented at `apps/api/src/engine/effects/continuous.ts:1170`, which gates custom-effect grants on a `beAffected` restriction with `byOpponentEffect: true`. Documented, untested here. |
| Q5069 | Gaining immunity ends an effect already applying | Not behaviourally reachable from this card without a persistent opposing aura fixture; the engine models it by recomputing continuous state each pass (`primitives.ts:4980` recomputes DP when a `beAffected` restriction changes). Documented, untested here. |
| Q5070 | Losing immunity re-applies the effect | Behavioural: "both halves last until the opponent's turn ends, and the same effect then lands" — inside the opponent's turn the suspend is blocked; after the turn loop carries play to the opponent's next turn, the same BT1-070 suspend lands. |
| Q5071 | A granted trigger does not fire while unaffectable | Same seam as Q5068 (`continuous.ts:1170` drops granted effects while the grantee is unaffectable). Documented, untested here — needs an opponent card that grants a triggered effect. |

| `untilOpponentTurnEnd` | Duration test through `startTurnLoop()` — both halves hold across the whole opponent turn and are gone at the start of my next turn. Not `ledger.sweep`. | `duration: "untilOpponentTurnEnd"` on both actions |

#### Defect fixed locally

`apps/api/src/cards/EX10/EX10-021.ts` used `match: "name"` for the bracketed `[Belphemon: Rage Mode]`
reference in both the `OnPlay` and `WhenDigivolving` costs. `match: "name"` is a **substring** match
(`matching/definition.ts:352`), so any future printing whose name merely contained
"Belphemon: Rage Mode" would have been an acceptable cost. The bracketed form is an exact-name
reference, and the peer printing of the identical clause, `apps/api/src/cards/BT13/BT13-088.ts`,
already uses `nameExact`. Changed both to `match: "nameExact"` and covered it with the
three-printings test. No card in the current catalog is a superstring of that name, so the defect
was latent, not live.

This changes the compiled IR, so `packages/shared/src/effects/effects.json` now drifts for
EX10-021 — the coordinator runs the sync.

#### Retained red (engine seam)

`it.fails("KB Q5067: an immune Digimon is still offered as a candidate for an opponent's effect")`.

- Seam: `apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/targeting/permanents.ts:138` (and `:507`) skip any
  permanent for which `isPermanentUnaffectable` (`interpreter/matching/permanent.ts:840`) is true,
  which resolves through `primitives.ts:5072` `isUnaffectableByOpponentEffects`.
- Expected (CR 15-15-5-3 / Q5067): an unaffectable permanent is still a legal choice for an
  opponent effect; it is simply not affected once chosen.
- Actual: it is filtered out of the candidate list, so the choice never appears.
- Impact: the end state matches the rules whenever another legal target exists or the effect has
  no follow-on clause. It diverges when the choice itself matters — e.g. "delete 1 of your
  opponent's Digimon. Then, <do X>" with one immune and one normal Digimon: by the rules the
  opponent may burn the effect on the immune one; here the effect is forced onto the normal one.
  Engine-wide, not card-local, so it was not fixed in this lane.

#### Remaining gaps

- Q5068, Q5069, Q5071 are documented, not tested (see the table). Each needs a fixture card that
  grants an effect to an opponent's Digimon; none is reachable from EX10-021's own text.
- "Suspend 2" is not proved against a board holding exactly one legal opponent permanent
  ("as many as possible"), because no ruling on this card fixes that case.
- The `[Opponent's Turn]` watcher is proved only through attack-declaration suspension. Effect-driven
  opponent suspension (`whenEffectSuspends` sibling path) is not exercised.

### EX10-022 — Belphemon: Rage Mode

#### Current revalidation (2026-09-12)

Independent Luna review of current catalog, KB, module and complete colocated
suite `apps/api/src/cards/EX10/EX10-022.test.ts`: Simultaneous Start Main ordering, hand threshold, suspend/delete boundaries, inherited mandatory trash, Sleep Mode host and Fortitude.

across the collection. The older report below is historical.

#### Card summary

DP 14000, Mega, Virus, `[Demon Lord]` `[Seven Great Demon Lords]`, SR, max 4.
Evo costs: Green Lv.5 for 6, Purple Lv.5 for 6. No Security effect.

Printed clauses:

2. `＜Fortitude＞`.
3. `[Start of Your Main Phase]` Suspend all of your opponent's level 5 or lower Digimon.

   suspended Digimon or Tamers.

   this Digimon's top stacked card.

Catalog agrees with the official text. No discrepancy found.

| Q5073 | The inherited `[End of Opponent's Turn]` trash is mandatory whenever possible. | Turn-loop test run with `autoDeclineOptional: true`: the top stacked card is still trashed and no optional decision is raised. |

#### What the previous audit missed

The committed test proved `[Start of Your Main Phase]`, `[On Play]`, `[When Digivolving]`
and the inherited clause **only** through injected timing (`advance().fireForPermanent` /
`advance().fire`) and proved `＜Fortitude＞` through `advance().verb.deletePermanent`. Under
this run's evidence standard that is structural, not behavioural, so the previous 10/10
Behaviour claim was unsupported. It also had no evolution negative, no `[On Play]` play from
hand, and no coverage of either Q&A id as a ruling (Q5072's ordering was never exercised).

The rewritten file uses **no** injected timing at all: every clause is driven by
`startTurnLoop` / `runOneTurn`, `playCard`, `digivolve`, `attack` and `respondDecision`.

#### Findings

- **No card defect found.** The module is faithful to the printed text and to both rulings.
  In particular the `selfTopHasText` gate correctly matches on `"name"`: a `"text"` match
  would fire on a `Belphemon: Rage Mode` host, because EX10-022's own `[Digivolve]` line
  contains the string `[Belphemon: Sleep Mode]`. That is now pinned by a public-route test.
- **Engine behaviour worth recording (not a defect, no red test).** The two
  `[Start of Your Main Phase]` effects only produce the `orderTriggers` decision when the
  Delete clause is *already* activatable, i.e. the opponent has a suspended Digimon or Tamer
  before the window opens. With no suspended target the Delete clause is not collected in the
  first activation tier (`apps/api/src/engine/effects/stack.ts`, `activationTiers` /
  `pickNext`), so it joins a later tier and resolves alone, with no choice offered. The
  observable result matches the ruling in every reachable case — the only order that differs
  is "delete first", which in that board state can delete nothing — so nothing is asserted
  red. The Q5072 tests construct the pre-suspended board that does raise the real prompt.

play cost 9, 15000 DP, Mega, Unknown attribute, `[Unidentified]` type, SR, ACE with
`overflowMemory: 5`, `evoCosts` Green Lv.6 / Black Lv.6 at 5 memory.

Printed clauses:

1. `[Digivolve] While you have [Ryoma Mogami], [Astamon]: Cost 7`
2. `[Hand] [Counter] ＜Blast Digivolve＞`
3. `[On Play] [When Digivolving] Suspend all other Digimon and Tamers.`
4. `[When Digivolving] [When Attacking] [Once Per Turn] Delete 1 of your opponent's suspended Digimon.`

#### Q&A ids covered

| Q&A | Ruling | Status |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q5074 | The two `[When Digivolving]` effects trigger simultaneously; the player chooses the activation order. | **Retained red** (`it.fails`). Engine raises no `orderTriggers` decision. |
| Q5075 | The `[All Turns]` effect also blocks unsuspending from start-of-turn effects, because the unsuspend phase includes them. | Covered behaviourally through the production turn loop, plus the phase-scope reading below. |

##### Q5075 phase scope — verified through the real turn loop

The prior audit recorded a "phase scope" correction for this card. It is correct, and it
is now proved without injected timing:

- `TurnStateMachine.activePhase()` sets `Phase.Active`, fires `EffectTiming.OnStartTurn`,
  and only then calls `unsuspendForActivePhase`. Start-of-turn effects therefore run
  **inside** `Phase.Active`.
- `GameEngine.unsuspendAllForSeat` and `unsuspendRebootForSeat` both skip a permanent
  carrying `unsuspendDuringUnsuspendPhase`.
- `apps/api/src/engine/effects/primitives.ts:3996` blocks the effect-driven `unsuspend`
  primitive when `state.phase === Phase.Active`, which is exactly the Q5075 sub-case
  (a start-of-turn effect that unsuspends), and leaves effect-driven unsuspension in any
  other phase legal.

The turn-loop test drives both seats' real unsuspend phases and asserts nothing but
Quartzmon flips, including an opposing ＜Reboot＞ Digimon (§16-11), which is the only
non-turn-player unsuspend path the phase has. The "no card exists" gap: no catalog card
prints a `[Start of Your Turn]` unsuspend, so that literal sub-case of Q5075 has no
behavioural fixture; the `Phase.Active` guard above is its evidence.

#### Clause -> test -> IR mapping

| Clause | IR | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Catalog + compiled shape | whole `compiled` | `matches the catalog and compiles the printed clause set` |
| Printed Lv.6 Green/Black route, cost 5 | `evoCosts` (catalog) | `digivolves from a %s base for the printed cost of 5` (AD1-024 Green, AD1-004 Black) |
| 1. Astamon alternate, cost 7, while you have Ryoma Mogami | `digivolutionRequirement[0]` `namesExact` + `controllerControls` | `the cost-7 alternate route needs an [Astamon] base AND [Ryoma Mogami] in play` — positive with `alternateRequirementIndex: 0` and 7 memory actually paid, plus two negatives (no Ryoma; wrong base name) |
| 2. `[Hand] [Counter] ＜Blast Digivolve＞` | `trigger: "Counter"`, `isFromHand`, `keywords` | `[Hand] [Counter] ＜Blast Digivolve＞ digivolves from hand for free in the counter window` — real `attack` -> `counterWindowOpened` -> `respondCounter`, stack identity asserted |
| 3. `[On Play]` suspend all others | `OnPlay` / `Suspend` / `excludeSelf` / `controllerDefault: "any"` | `[On Play] played from hand suspends every other Digimon and Tamer on both sides` — real `playCard` for 9 memory |
| 3. `[When Digivolving]` suspend all others | `WhenDigivolving` / `Suspend` | `[When Digivolving] suspends the board and deletes 1 suspended opposing Digimon` |
| 4. Delete 1 opponent suspended Digimon | `Delete`, `controller: "opponent"`, `suspended: true`, `kind: ["Digimon"]` | same test — Tamer survives, standing Digimon survives, exactly one trash entry |
| 4. `[Once Per Turn]`, shared across both timings | `frequency: "OncePerTurn"` + `sharedUseKey: "EX10-023/suspended-delete"` | `[Once Per Turn] is shared: ...` — real `startTurnLoop`: digivolve use, same-turn attack refused, opponent turn, own turn attack deletes again |
| 5. `[All Turns]` unsuspend lock | `AllTurns` / `Restrict` / `unsuspendDuringUnsuspendPhase` / `forTheTurn` | `[All Turns] Q5075: ...` (both seats' production unsuspend phases, including ＜Reboot＞), `[All Turns] control: ...` (same board without Quartzmon unsuspends normally), `[All Turns] leaves effect-driven unsuspension outside the unsuspend phase alone` |
| ACE ＜Overflow＞ 5 | catalog `overflowMemory` | `＜Overflow＞ 5 charges Quartzmon's own controller when it leaves the battle area` — real battle deletion, marker moves 5 toward the opponent (CR §4-18-2) |

Notes on the IR that I checked and accept:

- `duration: "forTheTurn"` is a real `EffectDurationRef`. The module comment is accurate:
  an `[All Turns]` static is re-derived on every continuous pass, so the lock persists.
  The rewritten suite proves this through two consecutive turn boundaries rather than by
  reading the ledger.
- `controllerControls: { kind: ["Digimon", "Tamer"], namesExact: ["Ryoma Mogami"] }` is the
  right reading of "While you have [Ryoma Mogami]" (CR 16-42-3): a Digimon or a Tamer
  with that name satisfies it. EX10-067 Ryoma Mogami is a Tamer, so the positive test only
  proves the Tamer half; no [Ryoma Mogami] Digimon exists in the catalog to prove the other.
- `Restrict` targets `controllerDefault: "any"` with `excludeSelf: true`, matching
  "Other than this Digimon, no Digimon or Tamers".

#### What the previous suite got wrong

The committed test file scored no behavioural or peer/stack credit under this brief:

1. **Injected timing.** `[On Play]`, both `[When Digivolving]` effects and `[When Attacking]`
   were proved only with `advance(...).fireForPermanent(...)`. The shared once-per-turn gate
   was never exercised through a turn boundary at all, so a `sharedUseKey` typo or a missing
   `frequency` would have passed.
2. **Seam violation.** Three tests cast
   `s.engine as unknown as { unsuspendForActivePhase(seat): Promise<string[]> }` to call a
   private `GameEngine` method directly. The brief forbids reaching past
   `apps/api/src/engine/testkit/`; the real unsuspend phase is reachable through
   `startTurnLoop()` + `advance(...).waitForMainPhase(seat)`, which is what the rewrite uses.
3. **Ledger reads standing in for behaviour.** The `[All Turns]` clause was asserted with
   `observe(...).isRestricted(...)` only — that proves a record exists, not that a permanent
   fails to unsuspend.
4. **No ACE proof.** Neither ＜Blast Digivolve＞ from hand in the counter window nor
   ＜Overflow＞ 5 on leaving the battle area was tested, although the brief requires both for
   an ACE card.
5. **No Q5074 test.** The ruling was asserted only in a source comment.
6. **Suspend-count blindness** (REVIEW-NOTES session-1 fixture fact): the old
   once-per-turn proof re-suspended candidates and could not tell a refused use from a
   repeated one.

None of this is a defect in `EX10-023.ts`. The module is correct as committed and I made
no module edits.

#### Retained red

`Q5074 raises an orderTriggers decision for the two simultaneous [When Digivolving] effects`
is `it.fails`.

- Seam name: `simultaneous-when-digivolving-ordering`. This is the `[When Digivolving]`
  face of REVIEW-NOTES engine seam 9 (`simultaneous-when-attacking-ordering`, raised by the
  EX10-009 lane against BT24-078).
- Files: `apps/api/src/engine/effects/subtriggers.ts` and the `fireTiming` /
  `fireSubTriggers` queue in `apps/api/src/engine/effects/EffectContext.ts`.
- Expected: digivolving into Quartzmon raises one `orderTriggers` decision to seat 0 whose
  `options.triggerKeys` lists both `[When Digivolving]` effects, per Q5074.
- Actual: `s.decisions` contains no `orderTriggers` request. The engine resolves the suspend
  effect and the delete effect in registration order and never offers the choice.
- Impact: the endpoint happens to be harmless for this card (suspend-then-delete is the
  order a player would usually pick, and the delete filter re-reads suspension at
  resolution), but the ruling is about player agency, so the test is held red rather than
  weakened.

#### Historical report (2026-09-08)

#### Card summary

Black Lv.3 Digimon, play cost 3, DP 1000, forms `Stnd.` / `Appmon`, attribute `System`,
type `Wallpaper`, link DP 2000.

Printed clauses:

1. `[Digivolve] Lv.2 w/[Appmon] trait: Cost 0` (alternate route; the printed `evoCosts`
   route is Black Lv.2 for 0).
2. `[Security] At the end of the battle, play this card without paying the cost.`

   ＜De-Digivolve 1＞ 1 of your opponent's Digimon.`

#### Q&A ids covered

| Id | Question | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q5076 | May the link card itself be trashed to pay its own link effect? Yes. | `Q5076: the link effect may trash Kabemon itself...` |
| Q5077 | With ＜Link +X＞, may another link card on the same host be trashed instead? Yes. | `Q5077: it may trash a different link card on the same host instead` |

#### Clause -> test -> IR

| Clause | Test | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Digivolve Lv.2 [Appmon] cost 0, plus the printed Black Lv.2 route | `digivolves for 0 from the printed Black Lv.2 route and from the [Appmon] Lv.2 route` (BT2-005 Kapurimon, EX10-001 Flickmon), negative: `refuses an illegal source` (BT1-009, Lv.3 Red) | `digivolutionRequirement: [{ level: 2, traits: ["Appmon"], cost: 0, isAlternate: true }]` |
| `[Link] [Appmon] trait: Cost 1` and +2000 link DP | `links only to an [Appmon] Digimon, for exactly 1 memory, and adds +2000 DP` (refused against non-Appmon BT1-009, accepted on BT21-009 Gatchmon) | `linkRequirement: [{ traits: ["Appmon"], cost: 1 }]` |
| `[Security]` free play | `[Security] battles first, then plays Kabemon without paying the cost` (memory unchanged at 4, security emptied, permanent on the opponent's board) | `trigger: "Security"`, `PlayWithoutCost` with `payCost: false` |
| Link `[When Attacking]` cost + ＜De-Digivolve 1＞ | Q5076, Q5077, plus `declining the optional cost leaves both the link card and the opposing stack untouched` | `trigger: "WhenAttacking"`, `isLinked: true`, `DeDigivolve` amount 1 with `cost.kind: "trash"`, `zone: "linked"`, `isSelfRef: true`, `optional`, `abortOnDecline` |

All link-effect proofs run from a real `attack` intent through the production attack flow
(attack declared, trigger, cost, De-Digivolve, security check settled, `isAttacking()` false).
No injected timing and no reach past the test seam.

#### What changed

- `EX10-024.test.ts` rewritten. The committed version proved both Q&A ids only with
  `advance(engine).fire(EffectTiming.OnUseAttack, ...)` (injected timing, no behavioural
  credit) and set up the two-link board with
  `advance(engine).ledgers.continuous.addLinkMaxGrant(...)` (past the seam, in
  `engine.continuous`). Both are gone:
  - the link effect now fires from `applyIntent(0, { type: "attack", ... })`;
  - the second link card is legal board state because the host prints ＜Link +6＞
    (BT26-086 Kiwamimon), so no ledger poke is needed.
- Added the illegal-source digivolve negative, the declined-cost branch, catalog text
  assertions and post-settle endpoints (stack contents, trash membership, `pendingDecision`,
  `observe(engine).isAttacking()`).
- `EX10-024.ts`: comment only. The old comment claimed the cost used `sameHost` plus
  `hostFilter`; neither field exists in the IR. The real mechanism is `zone: "linked"` with
  `isSelfRef: true`, which `interpreter/costs.ts` resolves to the SOURCE's host permanent's
  link cards — exactly "this Digimon's link cards", this card included. No IR change, so no
  `effects.json` drift.

#### Findings

- Implementation is faithful. No defect found in `EX10-024.ts`.
- Fixture trap worth recording for other lanes: `deDigivolve` in
  `apps/api/src/engine/effects/primitives.ts` has a `levelFloor` of 3, so a Lv.3 top card is
  never demoted. A ＜De-Digivolve＞ proof needs a Lv.4+ top (this test uses BT10-081 over
  BT10-074).
- A link card seeded past a host's link maximum is trashed by the rule sweep on the next
  attack, not at setup. `expect(host.linked).toHaveLength(2)` after `ready()` still passes,
  so a fixture that over-links silently loses the card mid-test. Using a host that prints
  ＜Link +6＞ avoids it without touching the ledger.

#### Catalog discrepancy (reported, not edited)

`packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json` writes EX10-024's `effectText` and
`linkRequirement` with U+00A0 (no-break space) between `[Appmon]` and `trait`:
`"[Digivolve] Lv.2 w/[Appmon] trait: Cost 0 "` and `"[Link] [Appmon] trait: Cost 1"`.
Official text uses a normal space. Nothing in the engine reads these strings, so it is
cosmetic, but any test asserting the printed text must use ` `.

#### Remaining gaps

- No test covers the link effect when the host has zero link cards, because Kabemon is
  itself a link card whenever the effect exists; the pool cannot be empty.
- The link intent is proved against one non-Appmon negative (BT1-009). A broader
  near-miss trait pool was not built; the requirement is a plain trait match.

```bash
  --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism --testTimeout=20000   # 9 passed (9)
pnpm --filter @aegis/api typecheck                            # clean
pnpm exec oxlint apps/api/src/cards/EX10/EX10-024.ts apps/api/src/cards/EX10/EX10-024.test.ts   # clean
pnpm exec oxfmt --check <same files>                          # correct format
git diff --check                                              # clean
```

Log: `docs/audits/EX10-reaudit/logs/EX10-024-vitest.log`,
`docs/audits/EX10-reaudit/logs/EX10-024-typecheck.log`.

### EX10-025 — Sunarizamon

#### Current revalidation (2026-09-12)

Independent Luna review of current catalog, KB, module and complete colocated
suite `apps/api/src/cards/EX10/EX10-025.test.ts`: Mineral/Rock conjunction, mandatory two-card cost/boundaries, refusal, bottom placement, inherited deletion and evolution carrier.
The 76-file collection baseline passed this suite. Card-level evidence credit:
catalog/rules 2/2, executable IR 2/2, behavioral proof 2/2, peer/stack 2/2;
delivery gates 2/2: **10/10**. Closing gates and committed delivery are shared
across the collection. The older report below is historical.

#### Historical report (2026-09-08)

#### Card summary

Black Lv.3 Rookie Digimon, Virus, `[Reptile] [LIBERATOR] [Mineral]`, play cost 3, DP 1000,
evolution requirement Black Lv.2 for 0 memory. No Security effect.

Printed clauses:

| # | Clause |
| --- | --- |
| C1 | `[On Play] You may place 2 cards with the [Mineral] or [Rock] trait from your trash as 1 of your [Mineral] or [Rock] trait Digimon's bottom digivolution cards.` |
| C2 | Inherited: `When effects trash this card from a [Mineral] or [Rock] trait Digimon's digivolution cards, delete 1 of your opponent's Digimon with a play cost of 4 or less.` |
| C3 | Evolution requirement `[Digivolve] Black Lv.2: Cost 0` (catalog `evoCosts`). |

#### Q&A covered

| Id | Ruling | Proof |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q5078 | With 2 or more eligible trash cards you must place 2; placing just 1 is illegal. | The selection decision is published with `min: 2, max: 2` over the 3 eligible cards, and a 1-card answer is refused by the engine (`{ ok: false, reason: "decision-pending" }`) with the host stack unchanged; the 2-card answer then resolves. |
| Q5079 | With only 1 eligible trash card you may place that single card. | With one `[Rock]` card in the trash the effect resolves and places it; no decision is left pending. |

#### Clause -> test -> IR

| Clause | Test | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| C1 quantity, source zone, trait gate, "bottom" position, "you may" | `Q5078: played from hand, the placement demands 2 cards and refuses a 1-card answer` | `OnPlay` -> `PlaceUnder` `from: ["trash"]`, `count: 2`, `position: "bottom"`, `optional: true`, `target.filter.nameOrTrait [Mineral, Rock] match "trait"` |
| C1 single-card case | `Q5079: with a single eligible trash card the effect places just that one card` | same action; `target.count: 1` is the preflight minimum only |
| C1 optionality | `declining the optional placement moves nothing and still leaves the Digimon in play` | `optional: true` |
| C1 no legal source | `offers no prompt at all when the trash holds no [Mineral] or [Rock] card` | `canAttemptPlaceUnder` preflight |
| C1 host filter | host-candidate assertion inside the Q5078 test (Landramon and the just-played Sunarizamon are offered, Monodramon is not) | `underFilter` `controller: "mine"`, `kind: ["Digimon"]`, `[Mineral]/[Rock]` |
| C2 positive + host gate | `inherited: deletes a cost-4-or-less opposing Digimon only from a [Mineral]/[Rock] host` | `Static/isInherited` -> `SubTrigger onDigivolutionCardsDiscardedBatch`, `hostFilter` `[Mineral]/[Rock]`, `Delete` `playCostLte: 4` |
| C2 "this card" gate | `inherited: stays silent when the same effect trashes OTHER cards off a [Mineral] host` | `sourceFilter: { isSelfRef: true }` |
| C3 legal route | `digivolves from a Black Lv.2 in breeding for 0 memory and keeps the egg as its source` | catalog `evoCosts` |
| C3 illegal source | `refuses an illegal evolution source: a Red Lv.3 is neither Black nor Lv.2` | catalog `evoCosts` |

All behaviour is driven by public intents only: `playCard` (both seats), `digivolve`,
`moveFromBreeding`, `respondDecision`. No `advance.fire*` injected timing is used anywhere in
the file, so every clause above earns behavioural credit. The inherited clause is driven by a
real opposing card, BT3-100 Death Parade Blaster ("[Main] Trash up to 2 digivolution cards from
the bottom of all of your opponent's Digimon"), played from the opponent's hand on their turn.

#### Commands and results

```
timeout 300 pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/EX10/EX10-025.test.ts \
  --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism --testTimeout=20000
-> Test Files 1 passed (1); Tests 9 passed (9)      [logs/EX10-025-vitest.log]

pnpm --filter @aegis/api typecheck   -> clean       [logs/EX10-025-typecheck.log]
pnpm exec oxlint <both files>        -> no findings
pnpm exec oxfmt --check <both files> -> correct format
git diff --check                     -> clean
```

Mutation checks (module reverted afterwards, module file is byte-identical to HEAD):

- `position: "bottom"`, `sourceFilter.isSelfRef` and `hostFilter` all removed at once ->
  3 tests fail (catalog/IR, Q5078 bottom placement, "this card" gate).
  [logs/EX10-025-mutation.log]
- `hostFilter` alone removed -> the inherited host-gate test fails (a second delete lands).
  [logs/EX10-025-mutation-hostfilter.log]

#### Defects found and fixed

The module needed no change; the previous test file was the defect.

1. **No behavioural proof at all.** Every previous clause test drove the card through
   `advance(...).fireForPermanent(EffectTiming.OnPlay, ...)` and
   `advance(...).verb.trashDigivolutionCards(...)` — injected timing, which the brief scores 0
   in the Behavior column. The card was never played, never digivolved and never reacted to a
   real opposing effect. Replaced by public-intent flows throughout.
2. **Q5078 was not proved.** The previous test set `autoSelectCards: true`, which always takes
   the maximum. That shows "2 can be placed", never "1 may not be". The new test asserts the
   published `min`/`max` bounds and that the engine refuses a 1-card answer.
3. **The host filter was not proved.** The previous negative gave the opponent one deletable
   Digimon, so a wrongly-firing second watcher had no legal target and the test still passed.
   Confirmed by mutation: dropping `hostFilter` left the old assertion green. The new fixture
   gives the opponent two play-cost-4-or-less Digimon plus a play-cost-6 one, so exactly one
   deletion is observable.
4. **The evolution route was `{ ok: true }` only.** Replaced with the real breeding route
   (hatch-position egg -> digivolve at 0 memory -> `moveFromBreeding`), asserting memory, the
   bonus draw, stack identity after each step, plus an illegal Red Lv.3 source negative
   (`{ ok: false, reason: "invalid-evolution" }`).

#### Catalog discrepancy

`cards.json` stores a NON-BREAKING SPACE (U+00A0) before each occurrence of the word "trait" in
both `effectText` and `inheritedEffectText` for EX10-025 (three occurrences). The official text
uses ordinary spaces. Not edited, per the brief; the test asserts the stored form with an
explicit ` ` escape and a comment. Any client-side text search for "[Rock] trait" with a
normal space will miss this card. Worth a catalog-wide normalisation pass by the coordinator —
EX10-028 carries the same inherited sentence and is likely affected too.

#### Remaining gaps

- The placement's card ORDER at the bottom of the stack is asserted as a set, not a sequence.
  `PlaceUnder` only reverses the selection when `order: "any"` is set, which the printed text
  does not grant, so no ruling fixes the relative order of the two placed cards.
- No test covers the inherited clause firing when the opponent has NO play-cost-4-or-less
  Digimon; the watcher would resolve with no legal target, which is uninteresting and untestable
  as an observable endpoint distinct from "did not fire".
- C1 is not exercised against a host in the breeding area. `underFilter` targeting defaults to
  the battle area, which matches the printed "your ... Digimon", so this is a non-gap.

#### Score

| Column | Score | Reason |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Catalog / rules | 2 | Every catalog field asserted, both Q&A ids proved, catalog NBSP discrepancy found and reported. |
| IR trace | 2 | Both clauses mapped to IR and to the engine paths that execute them (`runPlaceUnder`, `pickLoose`, `satisfiesMin`, `runTrashDigivolution`); three gates mutation-verified. |
| Behaviour | 2 | Every clause proved through public intents only; no injected timing in the file. |
| Peer / stack | 2 | Real evolution stack through breeding plus an illegal-source negative; mixed trait pool (Mineral, two Rock, near-miss Avian) and a non-matching host; peer EX10-028 used both as the host and as the identical-inherited-text comparison (its IR block matches this card's byte for byte); the inherited clause driven by a genuine opposing card effect. |
| Delivery gates | 0 | Worker lane. |
| **Total** | **8/10** | |

### EX10-026 — SkullKnightmon

#### Current revalidation (2026-09-12)

Independent Luna review of current catalog, KB, module and complete colocated
suite `apps/api/src/cards/EX10/EX10-026.test.ts`: Alternate evolution, both cost/delete timings, decline, Save bottom/refusal, inherited Blocker and DarkKnightmon DigiXros.
The 76-file collection baseline passed this suite. Card-level evidence credit:
catalog/rules 2/2, executable IR 2/2, behavioral proof 2/2, peer/stack 2/2;
delivery gates 2/2: **10/10**. Closing gates and committed delivery are shared
across the collection. The older report below is historical.

#### Historical report (2026-09-08)

Lane: card-only. Worktree `audit-ex10-reaudit`. Files touched: `apps/api/src/cards/EX10/EX10-026.ts`,
`apps/api/src/cards/EX10/EX10-026.test.ts`, this report, `docs/audits/EX10-reaudit/logs/EX10-026-*.log`.

#### Card summary

Black/Purple, Lv.4 Champion, Virus, [Undead]/[Bagra Army]/[Twilight]. Play cost 4, DP 4000.
EvoCost: Black Lv.3 cost 3, Purple Lv.3 cost 3.

Printed clauses:

1. `[Digivolve] Lv.3 w/[Knightmon] in text: Cost 2`
2. `[On Play] [When Digivolving] By trashing 1 card in your hand, delete 1 of your opponent's Digimon with a play cost of 4 or less.`
3. `[On Deletion] ＜Save＞`
4. Inherited: `＜Blocker＞`

The catalog record matches the official list. No discrepancy found.

#### Rules and Q&A

| Source | Bearing on this card |
| --- | --- |
| Q5080 | "[X] in its text" spans name, traits, effects, inherited effects, (Rule), digivolution / DNA / DigiXros / burst / App Fusion / Link / Assembly requirements. Applies to clause 1's `[Knightmon]` gate. |
| Comprehensive 4-22 | Same rule in the comprehensive text: "the information printed on that card". |
| Comprehensive 16-20 | ＜Save＞ places the card under 1 of your Tamers; the processing is optional. |
| Comprehensive 4-3 | A card placed under a Tamer that already holds cards goes to the **bottom**. |
| Comprehensive 15-7-4 | "By trashing 1 card in your hand, …" is an optional processing condition: the controller may decline even when it could be paid. So `optional: true` + `abortOnDecline: true` is the right shape, not a mandatory cost. |

Engine fact backing Q5080: `matchGatedRequirement` in `apps/api/src/engine/cards/cardData.ts`
builds the text gate from the union of effective names, types, forms, attributes and every
printed effect field. The catalog keeps the printed `[Digivolve]` / `[DigiXros]` headers inside
`effectText`, so requirement-line mentions are inside that union too.

#### Clause → test → IR mapping

| Clause | Test (`EX10-026.test.ts`) | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Catalog + alternate requirement present | "matches the catalog and compiles the printed alternate digivolution requirement" | `digivolutionRequirement[0]` |
| Clause 2 on play (public `playCard`), cost paid, play cost 4 boundary | "[On Play]: playing it from hand trashes 1 hand card and deletes a play cost 4 Digimon" | `effects[0]` OnPlay Delete + trash cost |
| Clause 2 declined (15-7-4) | "[On Play]: the optional processing condition may be declined…" | `optional`, `abortOnDecline` |
| Clause 2 on digivolve (public `digivolve`), normal Lv.3 route cost 3 | "[When Digivolving]: the normal Lv.3 route costs 3…" | `effects[1]` WhenDigivolving |
| Clause 1 positive (Q5080) | "[Digivolve] Lv.3 w/[Knightmon] in text: costs 2 off a Lv.3 naming [Knightmon]" | `digivolutionRequirement` |
| Clause 1 negative | "…a Lv.3 without [Knightmon] anywhere pays the printed cost" | same |
| Clause 3, bottom placement (4-3) | "[On Deletion] ＜Save＞: a battle deletion places it at the BOTTOM of a Tamer's stack" | `effects[2]` PlaceUnder `position: "bottom"` |
| Clause 3 optional (16-20-3) | "[On Deletion] ＜Save＞ is optional: declining sends it to the trash" | `optional: true` |
| Clause 4 | "inherited ＜Blocker＞: only the Digimon this card evolved into can block" | `effects[3]` inherited keyword |
| Cross-card DigiXros material identity | "DigiXros: it is a legal [SkullKnightmon] material for EX10-031, and a stranger is not" | EX10-031's `digiXrosRequirement` (read only) |

All proof runs through public intents: `playCard`, `digivolve` (with and without
`useAlternateCost`), `attack`, `declareBlock`, `playCard` with a `digiXros` plan, plus the real
turn loop for the block. No `advance.fire*` remains in this file — the previous test proved
clause 2 only through injected timing, which earns no behavioural credit.

Notable endpoint assertions: memory before/after every route (4→0 play, 3→0 normal digivolve,
3→1 alternate digivolve, 3→0 alternate refused, 7−2=5 DigiXros), exact trash and hand instance
ids, both battle areas by instance id, security length unchanged after the block, and
`pendingDecision === undefined` after every flow.

#### Defect found and fixed

**＜Save＞ placed the card at the top of the Tamer's stack, not the bottom.**
`runPlaceUnder`'s self/Save branch calls `relocateByEffect(..., { belowTop: action.position !== "bottom" })`.
The compiled IR carried no `position`, so `belowTop` defaulted to true and `pushOnStack`
appended the card — `Permanent.stack` is bottom-first, so the saved card landed directly beneath
the Tamer instead of under the existing cards. Comprehensive Rules 4-3 (and 16-21-6 for the
＜Material Save＞ analogue) require the bottom.

Fix: `position: "bottom"` on the OnDeletion `PlaceUnder` action in `EX10-026.ts`.

Revert check (`docs/audits/EX10-reaudit/logs/EX10-026-revert-check.log`): with the line removed,
the Save test fails with `expected [ 'inst-6', 'inst-3' ] to deeply equal [ 'inst-3', 'inst-6' ]`.
With it restored, all 10 tests pass.

Coordinator note: **this is systemic.** Every ＜Save＞ card in the repo compiles the same
positionless `PlaceUnder` (`BT10-020`, `BT10-021`, `BT10-075`, `BT11-015`, `BT11-077`,
`BT12-008`, `BT12-086`, `BT14-057`, `BT14-059`, `EX4-016`, `EX4-018`, `EX10-015`, `EX10-027`,
`EX10-039`, `EX10-044`, `EX10-045`, …). Only EX10-026 is fixed here; the rest need either the
same per-card edit or a compiler/interpreter default (the cleanest seam would be defaulting the
Save keyword's PlaceUnder to `position: "bottom"`).

#### Fixture defects in the previous test (corrected)

- The old cost-payment fixtures put `BT1-001` and `BT1-002` — both **Digi-Eggs** — in hand. Digi-Eggs
  cannot be in the main hand. Replaced with inert main-deck Digimon `BT1-013` / `BT1-014`.
- The old "play cost 4 or less" boundary used `BT1-009` (cost 2) versus `BT15-027` (cost 6), which
  never touches the 4/5 boundary. Replaced with `BT7-020` (cost 4, no printed text) versus
  `BT1-038` (cost 5, no printed text).
- The old alternate-route case asserted `{ok:true}` and memory 2→0, which is the same endpoint the
  printed EvoCost would produce at that memory; there was no negative base. Replaced with 3→1
  (alternate) and 3→0 (printed cost charged because the base has no `[Knightmon]`).
- The old inherited-Blocker case hand-laid the stack with `under:` and only read `hasKeyword`.
  Replaced with a real `digivolve` route plus a real `declareBlock` on the opponent's turn.

#### Remaining gaps

- **Q5080's name branch is not reachable for this card.** The gate only inspects Lv.3 bases, and the
  catalog holds no Lv.3 Digimon whose name (or trait) contains "Knightmon" — the only four Lv.3
  candidates (`BT7-057`, `BT10-058`, `BT18-058`, `BT19-055`) all carry it in `effectText`. The
  requirement-line branch is likewise unreachable at Lv.3. Both are covered by code reading
  (`matchGatedRequirement`'s text union) but not by a behavioural test from this card. The
  DigiXros case does prove the reverse direction — EX10-026's own name is matched exactly as the
  `[SkullKnightmon]` material — so the name axis is exercised, just not through clause 1.
- The `[On Play]` and `[When Digivolving]` clauses are two independent IR effects with identical
  bodies. Nothing proves they cannot both fire from one event; a single card cannot be both played
  and digivolved, so this is a structural duplication rather than an observable risk.
- No test asserts what happens when the controller accepts the optional condition with **no** legal
  delete target. 15-7-4 lets a player pay a condition whose follow-up cannot execute, and the KB has
  no ruling specific to this card, so the endpoint is left unasserted rather than guessed.

#### Commands and results

```text
timeout 300 pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/EX10/EX10-026.test.ts \
  --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism --testTimeout=20000
→ Test Files 1 passed (1) | Tests 10 passed (10)      logs/EX10-026-vitest.log

(revert check, position:"bottom" removed, -t "Save")
→ Tests 1 failed | 1 passed | 8 skipped               logs/EX10-026-revert-check.log

pnpm --filter @aegis/api typecheck
→ exit 2, ONLY from another lane's file:
  src/cards/EX10/EX10-027.test.ts(437,19) TS2322: '"digimon"' not assignable to '"player" | "permanent"'
  src/cards/EX10/EX10-027.test.ts(475,19) same
  No error in EX10-026.ts / EX10-026.test.ts.            logs/EX10-026-typecheck.log

pnpm exec oxlint apps/api/src/cards/EX10/EX10-026.ts apps/api/src/cards/EX10/EX10-026.test.ts → clean
pnpm exec oxfmt --check <same two files> → all matched files use the correct format
git diff --check → clean
```

`packages/shared/src/effects/effects.json` now drifts from the module (the added `position`).
The coordinator owns the sync; this lane did not edit it.

- **C2** `[On Play] [When Digivolving] By trashing 1 card in your hand, you may return 1
  Digimon card with [Knightmon] in its text or the [Bagra Army] or [Twilight] trait from
  your trash to the hand.`
- **C3** `[On Deletion] ＜Save＞`
- **C4** inherited `＜Retaliation＞`

No catalog discrepancy found.

#### Q&A ids covered

| Id | Ruling | Where proved |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q5081 | "[X] in its text" is the union of name, traits, effect / inherited / security / Link / DigiXros / digivolution-requirement text | `it.each(QUALIFYING_TRASH_CARDS)`, four rows, one per arm; plus the C1 route tests |
| Q5082 | You may pay the hand-card cost and still return nothing | `Q5082: may pay the hand-card cost and still return nothing` |

Q5081 arms proved with distinct fixtures so no row can pass for the wrong reason:

- `AD1-018` LordKnightmon — **name** substring only (traits Holy Warrior / Royal Knight).
- `BT18-058` Kotemon — **effect text** only (trait Reptile, name has no Knightmon).
- `BT10-070` Blastmon — **[Bagra Army] trait** only, no Knightmon anywhere.
- `BT10-060` Sparrowmon — **[Twilight] trait** only, no Knightmon anywhere.

Engine backing: `matchNameOrTrait` (`apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/matching/definition.ts`)
resolves `match: "text"` as names ∪ traits ∪ `effectText`/`inheritedEffectText`/
`securityEffectText`/`linkEffect`/`linkRequirement`/`dualEffect`/`optionEffect`. In this
catalog the alternate digivolution, DNA, DigiXros and App Fusion requirements are printed
inside `effectText`, so Q5081's full union is reachable. `match: "name"` would have missed
the effect-text arm and `match: "trait"` the name arm, so `"text"` is the right mode.

#### Clause → test → IR

| Clause | IR | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| C1 | `digivolutionRequirement: [{ level: 3, texts: ["Knightmon"], cost: 2, isAlternate: true }]` | `[Digivolve] Lv.3 w/[Knightmon] in text costs 2 and fires [When Digivolving]` (positive, memory 2→0, stack identity, bonus draw) and `refuses the alternate route from a level 3 source without [Knightmon] in its text` (negative on `BT2-052` Hagurumon, which is text-free, then the same source is accepted on the printed Lv.3 route at cost 3) |
| C2 | `OnPlay` and `WhenDigivolving` each carry the identical `Return` action: `filter { zone: trash, controller: mine, kind: [Digimon], nameOrTrait: [{Knightmon, text}, {[Bagra Army, Twilight], trait}] }`, `count 1`, `upTo true`, `to: "hand"`, `cost { kind: trash, hand, count 1 }`, `optional`, `abortOnDecline` | positive `[On Play]` path; the alternate-route `[When Digivolving]` path; four Q5081 rows; a non-matching-only negative; Q5082 pay-and-decline; `declining the optional pays no cost`; `with an empty hand the cost cannot be paid` |
| C3 | `OnDeletion` → `PlaceUnder { target isSelfRef+isSelf, underFilter { mine, Tamer, excludeToken }, position: "bottom", optional }` + `keywords [Save]` | `[On Deletion] ＜Save＞ places it under a Tamer after it loses a battle` (two cards already under `BT12-094` Yuu Amano, exact resulting order asserted), `＜Save＞ is declinable`, `＜Save＞ with no Tamer in play sends this card to the trash` |
| C4 | inherited `Static` effect with `keywords [Retaliation]` | `grants inherited ＜Retaliation＞ to its host only` (host true, standalone false) and `inherited ＜Retaliation＞ deletes the attacker when the host dies in battle` |

Every behavioural test drives the engine through public intents (`playCard`, `digivolve`,
`attack`, `respondDecision`) and the testkit seam only (`setupEngine`, `settle`, `observe`,
`perm`/`inst`, the decision-automation flags, `preferInstanceIds`). No injected timing
(`advance.fire`, `fireSubTrigger`, `fireTiming`) is used anywhere in this file, and nothing
reaches `engine.continuous`, `engine.ledger` or `engine.primitives`.

#### Defects found and fixed

**D1 — ＜Save＞ landed in the wrong stack position (fixed, load-bearing).**
The committed `OnDeletion` `PlaceUnder` carried no `position`. `runPlaceUnder`
(`apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/actions/placeUnder.ts:269`) computes
`belowTop: action.position !== "bottom"`, so a positionless action tucked the saved card
directly beneath the Tamer instead of at the bottom of the stack (comprehensive rules 4-3).
Added `position: "bottom"`, matching the accepted sibling `EX10-026.ts:76` and `EX10-025.ts`.

Proved load-bearing by mutation: replacing the `position: "bottom"` line with a comment and
re-running the file fails exactly one assertion for the intended reason —

```
× [On Deletion] ＜Save＞ places it under a Tamer after it loses a battle
AssertionError: expected [ 'inst-254', 'inst-255', 'inst-251' ] to deeply equal
                         [ 'inst-251', 'inst-254', 'inst-255' ]
```

(log: `logs/EX10-027-mutation.log`; the module was restored immediately afterwards.)

**D2 — the committed test was structural.** The `HEAD` version asserted IR shape and used
`EffectTiming` / `advance` injected timing, which earns no behavioural credit under this
brief. Replaced with the public-intent suite described above. The previous lane had already
begun that rewrite; I verified every fixture against the catalog (see the Q5081 table), kept
the parts that hold up, and added the two cases below.

#### Added this lane, on top of the previous lane's partial work

- `＜Save＞ with no Tamer in play sends this card to the trash` — the "may place under one of
  your **Tamers**" boundary: with no legal destination the card takes the ordinary deletion
  route and no decision is left pending.
- `inherited ＜Retaliation＞ deletes the attacker when the host dies in battle` — behavioural
  proof of C4 (the `observe(...).hasKeyword` case alone is a static read). It doubles as a
  stack-identity check: while EX10-027 is a digivolution card its own `[On Deletion]` ＜Save＞
  must stay silent, and the test asserts both cards reach the trash.

#### Remaining gaps and engine observations

**G1 — with no legal return target the cost is skipped as well.** In
`cannot return a Digimon that matches neither [Knightmon] text nor the two traits`, the
engine resolves the whole `Return` action to a no-op: no hand card is trashed. Q5082 says
the player *may* pay and return nothing, so refusing to pay is not itself illegal, but the
choice is being made for the player. It is observable: trashing a hand card feeds
trash-count effects (`AD1-018` reduces its play cost by 5 with four Knightmon-text cards in
the trash). This is an interpreter-wide `Return`-with-cost behaviour, not an EX10-027 defect,
so I recorded it rather than editing the shared seam. Seam:
`apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/actions/` return-action target resolution.

**G2 — no ACE / Security / DigiXros / Link surface.** The card prints none, so nothing is
untested there.

**G3 — the `abortOnDecline` outer prompt.** The printed "may" sits on the return, not on the
cost, and the engine additionally offers an outer accept/decline before the cost. That is
player-favorable and cannot produce an illegal board (declining is equivalent to not
activating), so I left it; it is why `declining the optional pays no cost` is a separate case
from the Q5082 case.

```
(mutation check, module restored afterwards)
→ Tests 1 failed | 16 passed (17)                    [logs/EX10-027-mutation.log]

→ 1 error, in another lane's file:
  src/cards/EX10/EX10-034.test.ts(368,19): TS2322 '"digimon"' not assignable to
  '"player" | "permanent"'. No error in EX10-027.ts or EX10-027.test.ts.
                                                      [logs/EX10-027-typecheck.log]

pnpm exec oxfmt --check <same two files>                                 → correct format
git diff --check                                                         → clean
```

No git write of any kind was performed; `git show HEAD:<path>` output was captured into

### EX10-028 — Landramon

#### Current revalidation (2026-09-12)

Independent Luna review of current catalog, KB, module and complete colocated
suite `apps/api/src/cards/EX10/EX10-028.test.ts`: Cross-stack Mineral/Rock cost, same-target grants/expiry, refusal, inherited deletion with reduced play cost Q5100.
The 76-file collection baseline passed this suite. Card-level evidence credit:
catalog/rules 2/2, executable IR 2/2, behavioral proof 2/2, peer/stack 2/2;
delivery gates 2/2: **10/10**. Closing gates and committed delivery are shared
across the collection. The older report below is historical.

#### Historical report (2026-09-08)

Lane: card-only worker. Base commit 46810fe1e. Module unchanged from HEAD; all edits are in
the colocated test.

#### Card summary

Catalog (packages/shared/src/cards/data/cards.json), verified field by field:

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| cardId / nameEn | EX10-028 / Landramon |
| colors / kinds | Black / Digimon |
| level / playCost / dp | 4 / 4 / 4000 |
| evoCosts | Black, Lv.3, 2 memory |
| forms / attributes / types | Champion / Virus / Mineral, LIBERATOR |
| rarity / maxCountInDeck | U / 4 |

Printed clauses:

1. `[On Play] [When Digivolving]` — By trashing any 1 card with the [Mineral] or [Rock] trait
   from your Digimon's digivolution cards, 1 of your Digimon with the [Mineral] or [Rock]
   trait gains ＜Reboot＞, ＜Blocker＞ and +3000 DP until your opponent's turn ends.
2. Inherited — When effects trash this card from a [Mineral] or [Rock] trait Digimon's
   digivolution cards, delete 1 of your opponent's Digimon with a play cost of 4 or less.

No Security effect. No printed digivolution requirement beyond the catalog EvoCost.

#### Q&A ids covered

| Id | Ruling | Where proved |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q5083 | The trash cost may come from another of your Digimon's digivolution cards. | `Q5083: [On Play] pays from another Digimon's stack…` — the cost card sits under `costHost`, a different permanent from the Landramon being played, and lands in trash. |
| Q5100 | The inherited delete reads the play cost after a reduction (6 − 2 = 4 qualifies). | `Q5100: the inherited delete reads the reduced play cost…` — both opposing Digimon are printed cost 6; a −2 adjustment on one makes exactly that one the deleted card. |

#### Clause → test → IR mapping

| Clause | IR | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `[On Play]` trigger | `effects[0].trigger = "OnPlay"` | Q5083 test, via the public `playCard` intent. |
| `[When Digivolving]` trigger | `effects[1].trigger = "WhenDigivolving"` | `[When Digivolving] from a Black Lv.3 for 2 memory…` via the public `digivolve` intent; stack identity, EvoCost 2 and the bonus draw all asserted. |
| Cost: trash 1 [Mineral]/[Rock] card from your Digimon's digivolution cards | `cost.kind = "trash"`, `from: ["digivolutionCards"]`, `filter.controller = "mine"`, `nameOrTrait match: "trait"`, no `kind` restriction ("any 1 card") | Q5083 test (exact trash contents, exact surviving stacks); `a [Rock Dragon] digivolution card cannot pay the cost…` for the negative. |
| Cost is optional and aborts the clause | `optional: true`, `abortOnDecline: true` on the first action | `declining the cost pays nothing and grants nothing` — stack intact, trash empty, no grants. |
| One chosen [Mineral]/[Rock] Digimon gains all three | action 1 `bindAs: "chosen"`, actions 2–3 `fromSelectionRef: "chosen"` | Q5083 test asserts DP, ＜Reboot＞ and ＜Blocker＞ on the same permanent and their absence on every other. |
| Exact trait gate, not substring | `match: "trait"` in both the cost and the target filter | `a [Rock Dragon] Digimon is not a legal target…` and the cost negative above. |
| "until your opponent's turn ends" | `duration: "untilOpponentTurnEnd"` on all three | `all three grants survive the opponent's whole turn and expire when it ends` — proved through `startTurnLoop()`, not `ledger.sweep`. |
| Inherited watcher, host trait gate | `Static` + `SubTrigger onDigivolutionCardsDiscardedBatch`, `sourceFilter.isSelfRef`, `hostFilter` trait-exact | `inherited: being trashed as a cost from a [Mineral] host deletes…`; negatives in `inherited: silent when trashed from a [Mini Dragon] host` and `… from a [Rock Dragon] host, the substring trap`. |
| Delete 1 opponent Digimon, play cost ≤ 4 | `Delete` with `playCostLte: 4`, `controller: "opponent"` | Inherited test seeds a cost-5 Digimon first in the preference order; it survives and the cost-4 one dies. Q5100 test covers the reduced-cost boundary. |
| Inherited watcher on a carrier built by public intents | same `SubTrigger` entry | `inherited: on a carrier stack built by playCard + digivolve…` — Landramon is played from hand, BT10-064 Gogmamon (Black Lv.5, [Rock]) digivolves onto it for EvoCost 3, and only then is it trashed as a second Landramon's `[On Play]` cost. No `under:` seeding anywhere in the fixture. |
| Inherited watcher is not restricted to your own effects | `sourceFilter` carries no seat restriction | `inherited: an opponent's BT1-099 Hearts Attack trash…` — the opponent plays a real Option that trashes all digivolution cards under a [Mineral] host; the watcher fires and deletes one of the caster's own play cost 4 Digimon. |
| EvoCost is Black Lv.3 only | catalog `evoCosts` | `refuses to digivolve from a Red Lv.3 source` and `… a Black Lv.4 source` — intent rejected, no zone, memory or hand movement. |

#### Coordinator trap check: substring over-match

The printed gate is "with the [Mineral] or [Rock] trait", which is exact. The module uses
`match: "trait"` in all three places (cost filter, target filter, inherited `hostFilter`) and
never a `texts` substring. The negative is proved, not asserted: BT2-011 Vorvomon and
BT2-014 Lavorvomon carry the trait `Rock Dragon`, which contains "Rock" as a substring. They
appear as a target candidate, as a digivolution card offered to the cost, and as an inherited
host, and are refused in all three roles.

Mutation proof: flipping every `match: "trait"` to `match: "traitContains"` in the module
turns three behavioural tests red plus the IR test
(`docs/audits/EX10-reaudit/logs/EX10-028-mutation-traitContains.log`). The module was
restored byte-for-byte from `git show HEAD` afterwards.

#### Round 2: what changed

Two Peer/stack gaps from round 1 were closed, and both existing routes were kept:

1. **The inherited carrier is now built through public intents.** `inherited: on a carrier stack
   built by playCard + digivolve…` plays Landramon from hand with the `playCard` intent (its own
   `[On Play]` cost is unpayable on an empty board, which is asserted), then digivolves BT10-064
   Gogmamon onto that permanent with the `digivolve` intent for its printed Black Lv.4 EvoCost 3,
   asserting the intermediate stack (`topCard` = Gogmamon, `stack` = [Landramon], DP 8000, memory
   0, the bonus draw in hand). A second Landramon is then played and its `[On Play]` trash cost
   takes the buried card out of that real stack. Endpoints asserted after settle: stack empty,
   trash holds exactly the buried instance, the opponent's play cost 4 Digimon is gone and the
   play cost 5 one survives (it is named first in `preferInstanceIds`, so a wrong ceiling would
   take it), the [Rock] host carries +3000 DP, ＜Reboot＞ and ＜Blocker＞, memory 0, exact hand,
   no `pendingDecision`.

2. **The inherited watcher is proved for an opponent-driven trash.** `inherited: an opponent's
   BT1-099 Hearts Attack trash…` has seat 1 play BT1-099 Hearts Attack ([Main] Trash all
   digivolution cards under 1 of your opponent's Digimon) through the public `playCard` intent.
   That is a genuine opponent effect: `runTrashDigivolution` passes `byEffectSeat: 1` into
   `trashDigivolutionCards`, which fires `onDigivolutionCardsDiscardedBatch`. Landramon's watcher
   fires against its own caster and deletes seat 1's play cost 4 Digimon while the play cost 5 one
   (named first in the preference list) survives. BT1-042 LoaderLeomon is on seat 1's board only
   to satisfy the blue Option's colour requirement; it is textless and play cost 7, so it never
   enters the delete pool.

##### ＜De-Digivolve＞ cannot reach this watcher (and should not)

The brief suggested EX8-049 Golemon or EX7-046 Jazarichmon as the opponent-driven trash. Neither
works, for a reason that is correct rather than a gap. `peelStackTops`
(`apps/api/src/engine/effects/primitives.ts`) trashes the permanent's **top card** and promotes the
card beneath it; it fires `whenDigimonTopTrashed`, never
`onDigivolutionCardsDiscardedBatch`. That matches the printed rule: a ＜De-Digivolve＞-trashed card
was the Digimon itself, not one of its digivolution cards, so Landramon's "when effects trash this
card from a … Digimon's digivolution cards" does not apply. BT1-099 is the right shape of probe.

##### Q5100 and the play-cost reduction

A catalog card that reduces an opponent's play cost does exist: **BT16-060 Tankdramon**
("… reduce the play costs of all of your opponent's Digimon by 1 until the end of their turn"),
compiled as a `CostModifier` with `mode: "reduce"`, `costType: "play"`, `controller: "opponent"`.
It was not used as the Q5100 probe: the same `[On Play]` also carries its own
`Delete … playCostLte: 4` against the opponent, resolving in the same window as the reduction it
creates, so a board that ends with one fewer opposing Digimon cannot be attributed to Landramon's
inherited delete rather than Tankdramon's own. The seeded
`advance(engine).ledgers.modifiers.addPlayCostAdjustment` isolates the boundary instead, and the
trash that fires the watcher in that test is still a public `[On Play]` cost. **The ledger arming
is therefore the only clean route to the Q5100 boundary in this catalog**, and it stays inside the
test seam.

#### Fixtures

All fixture cards were re-checked in cards.json and are textless (no effect, no inherited, no
security text): BT4-065 Gotsumon (Black Lv.3 [Rock], cost 4), BT10-062 Golemon (Black Lv.4
[Mineral], cost 5), BT10-064 Gogmamon (Black Lv.5 [Rock], cost 5), BT2-011 / BT2-014
([Rock Dragon]), BT1-019 DarkTyrannomon and BT3-067 Tankmon (both cost 6, the Q5100
boundary), BT1-042 LoaderLeomon (Blue Lv.5, textless, play cost 7 — the colour enabler for
BT1-099), plus BT1-009 / BT1-013 / BT1-014 as inert deck and security. No Digi-Egg in deck or
security; no numeric `security: <n>` form.

The cancelled lane left the test file rewritten and the module identical to HEAD. Its work was
verified rather than trusted: every fixture fact in its header comment was re-checked against
the catalog and is correct, and the mutation run above proves its trait negatives really bite.
Two gaps were found and filled in this lane: no illegal digivolution source negative existed,
so `refuses to digivolve from …` was added for a Red Lv.3 and a Black Lv.4 source. Formatting
was also out of date and was fixed with oxfmt.

Printed clauses:

2. `＜Blocker＞`

4. Link effect: `[When Linking] By trashing 1 of this Digimon's link cards, ＜De-Digivolve＞
   effects don't affect 1 of your Digimon until your opponent's turn ends.`

No `[Digivolve]` line, no inherited effect, no separate Security text field.

#### Q&A ids covered

| Id | Ruling | Covered by |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q5084 | The link effect may trash **this card itself** while it is a link card. | `Q5084 trashes Warpmon itself and protects exactly the selected Digimon` |
| Q5085 | With ＜Link +X＞, it may trash **another** of the same host's link cards. | `Q5085 trashes another same-host link, never a neighboring link, and may decline` |

Both are proved through the public `linkCard` intent with `preferInstanceIds` steering the
cost choice; the Q5085 board gets its second link slot from BT21-101 Gaiamon's printed
＜Link +1＞, not from a ledger grant.

#### Clause -> test -> IR

| Clause | IR | Test |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `[Security]` end-of-battle self-play | `trigger: "Security"`, `timing: "endOfBattle"`, `isSecurity: true`, `SubTrigger whenSecurityBattleEnded (once)` -> `PlayWithoutCost from ["trash"], payCost:false` | `Security plays Warpmon for free and the resulting permanent has Blocker` — the 3000 DP attacker dies to Warpmon's 4000 DP security battle, security empties, Warpmon then arrives on seat 1's board unsuspended, memory unchanged at 4 |
| `＜Blocker＞` | `Static` -> `GainKeyword Blocker`, `duration: "permanent"` | `＜Blocker＞ really blocks…` — `declareBlock` redirects the attack, both aliased security cards (`secTop` BT1-013, `secBottom` BT1-014) stay in place and in order, attacker trashed, Warpmon suspended |
| Evolution route (catalog evoCost only) | no `digivolutionRequirement` | `evolves for 2 on any black level-3 base…` — BT10-058 (Black, non-Appmon) and BT21-053 (Black, Appmon) both accept for 2 memory, source card becomes the digivolution card, DP 4000; BT1-009 (Red) is rejected |
| `[Link] [Appmon] trait: Cost 2` | `linkRequirement: [{ cost: 2, traits: ["Appmon"] }]` | same test — link onto BT21-009 (Appmon) costs 2 and adds +3000 DP; link onto BT1-009 is rejected |
| `[When Linking]` cost + restriction | `Static, isLinked:true` -> `SubTrigger whenLinked` (`sourceFilter isSelfRef`, `optional`, `cost: trash {zone:"linked", isSelfRef:true}`) -> `SelectBind A` -> `Restrict cantBeDeDigivolved, untilOpponentTurnEnd` | Q5084, Q5085, `the De-Digivolve protection lasts through the opponent's turn and expires at its end`, and `a real ＜De-Digivolve＞ cannot demote the protected Digimon…` |

`zone: "linked"` + `isSelfRef: true` is read by `costs.ts` as "the link cards of the SOURCE's
host permanent" — exactly "this Digimon's link cards", Warpmon itself included. This is the
same shape the accepted EX10-024 Kabemon lane used for Q5076/Q5077, the direct analogue of
Q5084/Q5085.

#### Defects found and fixed locally

1. **Security timing (carried over from the cancelled lane, verified and kept).** HEAD compiled
   the `[Security]` clause as an immediate `PlayWithoutCost` with no `timing` and no `from`.
   That pulls the card out of security *before* the security battle, so the battle never
   happens. The worktree version now matches the canonical shape used by every other
   "At the end of the battle, play this card" printing (BT21-015, BT23-052, BT18-035):
   `timing: "endOfBattle"` + `SubTrigger whenSecurityBattleEnded (once)` +
   `PlayWithoutCost from: ["trash"]`. Test asserts the attacker dies in the security battle
   and only then Warpmon arrives.
2. **Test reached past the seam (fixed by the cancelled lane, verified).** The committed test
   built its multi-link board with `advance(accepted.engine).ledgers.continuous.addLinkMaxGrant(...)`
   (HEAD copy line 181, saved in `logs/EX10-029.test.ts.HEAD`). It now uses BT21-101 Gaiamon's
   printed ＜Link +1＞ instead, and the test additionally asserts Gaiamon's own
   "when your Digimon get linked" clause fired (host unsuspended, opponent security 2 -> 1),
   which proves the host really held two link cards at once.
3. **Restriction was proved only as a flag.** Every previous assertion was
   `observe(...).isRestricted(perm, "cantBeDeDigivolved")`. That is engine state, not
   behaviour. Added `a real ＜De-Digivolve＞ cannot demote the protected Digimon, but demotes it
   without the link`: inside the real turn loop, the opponent plays EX7-046 Jazarichmon
   (`[On Play] ＜De-Digivolve 1＞ 1 of your opponent's Digimon`) against a Lv.5 BT10-081 over
   Lv.4 BT10-074 stack. With Warpmon linked the stack is untouched (`BT10-081` still on top,
   `[BT10-074]` still underneath, seat 0 trash holds only the spent Warpmon). The control run
   is the identical board and identical opponent play with Warpmon never linked: the Lv.5 top
   is trashed and BT10-074 becomes the top. The Lv.4 floor is respected (the brief's
   `levelFloor = 3` note), so this is a real demotion, not a floor no-op.
4. **Catalog quirk found.** `linkRequirement` separates `[Appmon]` from `trait` with a
   non-breaking space (U+00A0), not a normal space. Not edited (catalog is read-only for this
   lane); the test normalises before comparing and the report records it.
5. **Numeric `security: <n>` fixtures removed (coordinator correction).** The ＜Blocker＞ test
   used `security: 1` / `security: 2`, which the brief forbids. Both are now explicit inert
   main-deck Digimon arrays (`BT1-013`, `BT1-014`), and the "no security was checked"
   assertion was upgraded from a length check to an exact aliased instance-id order check.
6. **Assertions strengthened.** Added digivolution-stack identity and DP after each evolution
   route, and explicit `inheritedEffectText`/`securityEffectText` absence.

The invented `digivolutionRequirement` alternate `{cost: 2, traits: ["StandardApp"]}` was
already removed at HEAD; `StandardApp` is not a trait any catalog card carries. Confirmed and
asserted (`compiled.digivolutionRequirement` is undefined).

#### Retained reds

None. No `it.fails` / `it.skip` in this lane.

#### Remaining gaps

1. **No opponent-controller negative for the `SelectBind` pool.** The restriction filter is
   `controller: "mine"`, and the tests assert a second *own* Digimon is left unrestricted, but
   no case forces the choice against an opponent's Digimon. The host permanent is always a
   legal own candidate, so a board where only opponent Digimon are selectable cannot be built
   through public intents.
2. **IR shape inconsistency across the set (not a defect here).** This card models
   `[When Linking]` as `trigger: "Static", isLinked: true` + `SubTrigger whenLinked`. Peers use
   `AllTurns` (BT23-033) or `YourTurn` (BT23-021) with the same SubTrigger, and BT23-021 also
   carries a native `trigger: "WhenLinking"` effect alongside it. Behaviour here is proven
   correct through public intents; the divergence is a coordinator-level normalisation
   question, not an EX10-029 bug.
3. **Warpmon is never itself digivolved into.** Its behaviour as a digivolution card under a
   Lv.5 top is not exercised; it prints no inherited effect, so there is nothing to assert.

#### Q&A ids covered

| Id | Ruling | Where proved |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q5086 | The link effect may pay by trashing this card itself when it is a link card. | `[Once Per Turn] resets…` — the second save pays with Cometmon, the only link card left. |
| Q5087 | The free link cannot choose a card without `<Link>`. | `Q5087: a card without <Link> is not a legal target…` — a hand holding only BT1-009 links nothing and stays in hand. |
| Q5088 | A link card trashed because a new link replaces it does not trigger the `[All Turns]` clause. | `Q5088: a link card trashed by the over-limit replace rule…` — the opponent Digimon stays at 20 000 DP. |
| Q5089 | With `<Link +X>` the effect may trash a link card other than this card. | `Q5089/Q5086: the link replacement pays with another of the host's link cards…` — on a BT21-101 Gaiamon (`＜Link +1＞`) the cost trashes BT24-036, not Cometmon. |

#### Clause → test → IR mapping

| Clause | Test | IR |
| --- | --- | --- |
| App Fusion Warpmon & Weatherdramon, cost 0 | `[App Fusion] … through the public appFusion intent` (public `appFusion` intent, memory stays 0, both materials become digivolution cards, bonus draw) | `appFusionRequirement: [{ names: ["Warpmon","Weatherdramon"], cost: 0 }]` |
| `＜Collision＞` | `＜Collision＞ forces the opponent to block…` — `blockWindowOpened { mustBlock: true }`, `declineBlock` refused | `Static` effect, `keywords: [Collision]` |
| Free link, On Play | `[On Play] links a level-4-or-lower Digimon card with <Link> from hand, free of its link cost` — memory drops 10→2 (play cost only, Medicmon's Cost 2 unpaid) | `OnPlay` → `Link`, `payCost: false`, `optional: true` |
| Free link, When Digivolving, from digivolution cards | `[When Digivolving] fires on the printed black Lv.4 route…` and `also digivolves on the printed yellow Lv.4 route…` — the source card moves from the stack into the link zone | `WhenDigivolving` → same `Link` action, `from: ["hand","digivolutionCards"]` |
| Level ≤ 4 and `<Link>` filter | `Q5087…` | `levelComparison { op: "lte", value: 4 }`, `hasLinkRequirement: true` |
| `[All Turns][OPT]` -8000 on link trash | `＜Collision＞…` (victim 20 000 → 12 000, back to 20 000 after the turn ends through the real turn loop) and `Q5088…` (negative) | `AllTurns` → `SubTrigger whenLinkTrashed` → `ModifyDP -8000 forTheTurn`, `frequency: OncePerTurn` |
| Lower-box would-leave replacement (positive) | `＜Collision＞…`, `Q5089/Q5086…`, `[Once Per Turn] resets…`, `may decline the replacement…` | `AllTurns` → `Replacement wouldLeavePlay` → `Prevent leavePlay` with a `trash` cost on `{ isSelfRef: true, zone: "linked" }`, `optional: true`, `abortOnDecline: true`, `frequency: OncePerTurn` |
| …granted from the link zone only | `grants no would-leave replacement while Cometmon is a digivolution card instead of a link card` | `isLinked: true` on that effect. The kernel gates on it (`engine/effects/kernel.ts:135`, `if (effect.isLinked) return wasLinkedCard`), which is the shape BT23-052 and EX10-029 use. |
| Once per turn identity | `Q5089/Q5086…` (same-turn second would-leave is not prevented; host leaves) and `[Once Per Turn] resets…` (saved again after a full turn cycle) | `frequency: OncePerTurn` |
| "may" | `may decline the replacement…` with `autoDeclineOptional` — host leaves, no link card trashed for the cost | `optional: true` |

All behavioural proof runs through public intents (`playCard`, `digivolve`, `appFusion`,
`attack`, `declareBlock`, `declineBlock`, `surrender`) and `startTurnLoop()`. No test reaches
`engine.continuous`, `engine.ledger` or `engine.primitives`. Duration expiry is proved by ending
the turn through the loop, not by sweeping the ledger.

#### Defects fixed in this lane

1. **Test seam breach (from the committed file).** The committed test called
   `advance(s.engine).ledgers.continuous.addLinkMaxGrant(...)` twice to fabricate a multi-link
   board. Replaced with BT21-101 Gaiamon, which prints `＜Link +1＞`, so the two-link board is
   real. (Committed copy kept at `logs/EX10-030.test.committed.ts`.)
2. **Broken fixture in the digivolve test.** It used EX10-029 Warpmon as the Lv.4 source. Warpmon's
   link effect is `[When Linking] By trashing 1 of this Digimon's link cards, …`, so accepting it
   immediately trashed the card the test had just linked; the assertion saw an empty link zone.
   Replaced with BT23-052 Consulmon, whose link effect only grants keywords.
3. **Wrong sort order** in the decline test (`[CARD_ID, "EX10-029"]` is not the sorted order).
4. **Leftover `console.log("DEBUG …")` statements** removed.
5. **Catalog assertion corrected** for the non-breaking space (below).
6. **Added** a Yellow Lv.4 digivolution route test, so both printed evo costs are exercised.
7. **Residency of the lower box corrected** (coordinator follow-up). The module recorded the
   would-leave replacement as `isInherited: true`, which also granted it while Cometmon sat in a
   host's *digivolution stack* — behaviour the printed card does not have. It is now
   `isLinked: true`, matching the corrected catalog and the 76 other link cards. The stale module
   comment defending the inherited reading was rewritten.

#### Catalog discrepancies (reported, not edited)

1. `linkRequirement` is `"[Link] [Appmon] trait: Cost 3"` — a **non-breaking space (U+00A0)**
   between `[Appmon]` and `trait`, where every peer Appmon card uses a normal space. The test now
   asserts the literal ` ` so the mismatch is visible rather than silent. BT26-028 has the
   related defect of a trailing space. Worth a catalog cleanup pass.
2. **Fixed by the coordinator.** The lower box sat in **`inheritedEffectText`**, but the catalog has a dedicated `linkEffect`
   field and every other Appmon link card uses it (EX10-029, BT23-052, BT24-036, …). KB Q5086 and
   Q5089 both call this clause "this card's **link** effect". EX10-030 is the only card in the
   catalog with a `linkRequirement` and an `inheritedEffectText` but no `linkEffect`. This looks
   like a scrape error. The catalog now carries it as `linkEffect` and drops
   `inheritedEffectText` for this card; the test asserts both.

#### Remaining gaps

- **Removed fabricated clause.** A `[When Attacking] … return 1 [Appmon] Digimon card from your
  trash` clause was deleted from this module before this lane. It appears nowhere in the catalog or
  KB, and the deletion is right. Note that EX10-014 Weatherdramon's real link effect has a similar
  shape (`[When Attacking] By trashing 1 of this Digimon's link cards, …`), which is the likely
  source of the mix-up. The only guard is a shape assertion (`no Return action`), not behaviour.
- **`-8000` target choice** is proved with a single opponent Digimon; a two-candidate board that
  proves the controller chooses is not covered.
- **Link cost boundary** (a Lv.5 card refused as a link target) is not covered: no Lv.5 catalog card
  carries a `linkRequirement` and is inert enough to be a safe fixture.
- **`＜Collision＞` negative** (an ineligible blocker not being forced) is not covered.

##### IR keys verified against the engine

- `SelectBind` / `Target.fromSelectionRef`: real IR
  (`packages/shared/src/effects/ir/actions/combat.ts:93`, `action.ts:209`). The pre-existing

  `ModifyDP.fromSelectionRef` are unread; the current shape is the typed one.

  restriction with this flag is skipped when the acting effect is the restricted
  permanent's own controller's. Matches the printed "THEIR ＜De-Digivolve＞ effects".

  honoured by `apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/targeting/loose.ts:312-332`.
  `interpreter/actions/play.ts` supplies a self host scope ONLY for ＜Decode＞ plays
  (`applyDecodeHostScope`), so the explicit `hostFilter` here is load-bearing — proved by
  the bystander assertion.
- `duration: "untilOpponentTurnEnd"` is a real `EffectDurationRef` (used widely, e.g.
  `apps/api/src/engine/effects/capabilities.test.ts`).

   identical Lv.4 EX10-028 stacks — one protected, one not. Both the block and the peel

2. Added a control test: the same fixture with the protection placed on a DECOY, the
   opponent aiming at the same stack, which then peels. This closes the "did the aim even
   land there?" hole in the negative assertion.
3. Added the inherited-redirect [Once Per Turn] same-turn refusal: attacker A is
   redirected into the 12000 DP host and dies with no security check; attacker B, same
   turn, reaches security.
4. Added the [Once Per Turn] reset through the real turn loop (`startTurnLoop`,
   `waitForMainPhase`, `endMainPhaseIfOpen`). On the opponent's first turn attacker A is
   redirected (security 4 -> 4, attacker deleted) and attacker B is NOT (security 4 -> 3).
   The loop then crosses my whole turn and re-enters the opponent's next turn, where
   attacker C is redirected again: security unchanged, attacker deleted, host still on the
   board. Intermediate state is asserted at every step.
5. Corrected the module comment that named a nonexistent file
   (`targeting/loose.ts`) and now records the ＜Decode＞-only default in `play.ts`.

No engine, shared, catalog or sibling-card file was touched. No git write of any kind.

   card under audit and fires it with `advance.fireForPermanent`, restoring the original
   record in a `finally`. Injected timing: no behavioural credit claimed.
2. **[All Turns] [Once Per Turn] on the would-leave replacement.** Cannot fire twice
   through public intents: the Digimon leaves the battle area once and does not come
   back, so no legal same-turn second trigger exists. Documented, not tested — the same
   reasoning the coordinator accepted for `whenMovedFromBreeding` gates.
3. **DigiXros duplicate material.** CR 7-2-2-5 allows only one of each specified card;
   placing two SkullKnightmon is not asserted. Low risk, untested.

### EX10-032 — Proganomon

#### Current revalidation (2026-09-12)

Independent Luna review of current catalog, KB, module and complete colocated
suite `apps/api/src/cards/EX10/EX10-032.test.ts`: Hand/Main placement evolution, cross-stack cost, grants/expiry, inherited De-Digivolve, Q5091–Q5093 and P-107 peer regressions.
The 76-file collection baseline passed this suite. Card-level evidence credit:
catalog/rules 2/2, executable IR 2/2, behavioral proof 2/2, peer/stack 2/2;
delivery gates 2/2: **10/10**. Closing gates and committed delivery are shared
across the collection. The older report below is historical.

#### Historical report (2026-09-08)

Black, Lv.5 Ultimate, Virus, [Mineral]/[LIBERATOR], play cost 7, DP 7000,
digivolve: Black Lv.4 for 3. No security effect. Rarity C, max 4 per deck.

#### Printed clauses

1. `[Hand] [Main]` If you have [Close], by placing 1 [Landramon] from your trash as any of
   your [Sunarizamon]'s bottom digivolution card, it digivolves into this card for a
   digivolution cost of 3, ignoring digivolution requirements.
2. `[On Play] [When Digivolving] [When Attacking]` By trashing any 1 [Mineral] or [Rock]
   trait card from your Digimon's digivolution cards, 1 of your such Digimon gains
   ＜Collision＞, ＜Piercing＞ and +3000 DP until your opponent's turn ends.
3. Inherited: when effects trash this card from a [Mineral] or [Rock] trait Digimon's
   digivolution cards, ＜De-Digivolve 1＞ 1 of your opponent's Digimon.

Catalog text and the official list agree. No discrepancy found.

#### Q&A ids covered

| Q5091 | Combining clause 1 with BT21-055 Sunarizamon's own reduction makes the digivolution cost 2. | `Q5091 places Landramon under Sunarizamon and hand-digivolves for the reduced cost 2` — memory 3 → 1 after the real `activateEffect`. |
| Q5092 | Clause 1 cannot be activated together with a digivolving effect such as P-107 Defense Training. | Three tests. `Q5092 the [Hand] [Main] clause is self-contained` (P-107 dormant on the board, ordinary `digivolve` still refused). `Q5092 P-107's ＜Delay＞, played and activated publicly` — P-107 reaches the board through `playCard`, its ＜Delay＞ is activated on a LATER turn through `activateEffect`, and the digivolution it grants is the ordinary one (legal Lv.4 Black source only). `Q5092 negative` — the same activated ＜Delay＞ cannot put this card onto a Lv.3 [Sunarizamon], and clause 1 then still resolves on its own. Two of the three are red on a P-107 defect; see "Defects and seams". |
| Q5093 | Clause 2 may trash a digivolution card from another of your Digimon. | Three tests pay the cost from a stack under a different Digimon: the `[On Play]`, the `[When Attacking]` and the "such Digimon" tests. |

only failing assertion in each is the memory endpoint. So every other endpoint in the two red
tests is green and the reds pin exactly the cost defect. Log: `logs/EX10-032-red-isolation.log`.
