| `EX10-033` "declining both optional clauses …" | **This lane** (seam 19). The assertion encodes the pre-fix all-or-nothing cost; see seam 19 above for the evidence and the card-lane follow-up. |

### EX10 re-audit — the 36 non-settle failures

Source: `docs/audits/EX10-reaudit/NON-SETTLE-FIXES.md`.

Scope: every line in `docs/audits/EX10-reaudit/logs/post-wave2-failures.txt` that does **not**
contain `settle: predicate never held`. Three groups: persisted-IR drift, stale link-DP test
baselines, and one seam-9 ruling change.

#### Method — the pristine HEAD baseline

To separate "this session broke it" from "it was already red at HEAD", the 11 files were run
against an unmodified copy of `HEAD`:

```
mkdir -p docs/audits/EX10-reaudit/logs/pristine
git archive HEAD | tar -x -C docs/audits/EX10-reaudit/logs/pristine
# node_modules symlinked from the worktree (root, apps/api, packages/shared);
# @aegis/shared built inside the copy so the tests read the copy's effects.json.
```

Pristine HEAD result:

```
 Test Files  8 failed | 3 passed (11)
      Tests  33 failed | 970 passed (1003)
```

The pristine tree was deleted after the run.

| Failure | Red at HEAD? |
| --- | --- |
| BT10 / BT11 / BT12 / BT14 / BT19 catalog-sync (30 tests) | yes — pre-existing |
| `BT22-009` link DP, `BT23-009` link DP, `appFusionLinkPlacement` | yes — pre-existing |
| `EX10-catalog-sync`, `promo-lm-rb.catalog-parity` (P-107) | no — caused by this session's card fixes |
| `irKindTier2Cluster` Tier-2 A3 | no — caused by seam 9 |

#### 1. Persisted IR out of sync (32 tests)

`packages/shared/src/effects/effects.json` had drifted from the authoritative card modules.
Resynchronised with the repo tool, one set at a time:

```
node tools/sync-effects-from-card-modules.mjs --set <SET>
```

Sets the tool reported stale and synchronised: **BT10, BT11, BT12, BT14, BT19, BT21, EX10, P**.
BT1 was checked as well (the seam-15 note flagged it) and reported `BT1: 115 records already
synchronized` — a sibling agent had already resynced it.

The whole diff is 96 insertions / 65 deletions and every hunk traces to a card module or to a
landed engine change:

- **36 x `"position": "bottom"`** on the `PlaceUnder` action of ＜Save＞ effects. This is the
  committed normalizer at `apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/registration/normalize.ts:105`
  (landed in `0c247d8c9`, "close EX10 audit seams in save, costs and triggers"); the persisted IR
  was simply never resynced after it. `apps/api/src/engine/effects/saveKeywordPlacement.test.ts`
  is the unit proof of that default.
- **`BT1-070`** gained `allowUnaffectableChoice: true` — already present in the worktree before
  this work (a sibling agent's BT1 fix), carried through unchanged.
- **`EX10-052`** — the `trash 1 card in your hand` cost moved from the `Delete` action up to the
  clause, matching this session's `EX10-052.ts` fix.
- **`P-107`** — the `Replacement` / `wouldDigivolve` / `reduceCost` sub-block collapsed into
  `reduceCost: 2` plus `allowNoTarget: true` on the `Digivolve` action, matching this session's
  `P-107.ts` fix (`P-107-FIX.md`).

No drift was persisted that does not come from a card module.
`pnpm --filter @aegis/shared build` was rerun afterwards.

#### 2. Stale link-DP test baselines (3 tests)

All three were red at pristine HEAD. They are fallout from the committed **seam 7 part 2**
(`setupEngine` now runs `recomputeDP` for seeded links — see `ENGINE-LANE-1.md`, "Seam 7").
Before that change a seeded link contributed no DP, so each test's baseline recorded the bare
printed DP. The engine is right in all three; the test baselines were wrong.

| Test | Was | Now | Arithmetic |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `BT22-009.test.ts` "adds the printed 3000 link DP" | `8000` | `11000` | BT22-035 Entermon prints 8000 DP + BT22-009 Effecmon's printed 3000 link DP |
| `appFusionLinkPlacement.test.ts` | `4000` | `6000` | BT26-051 Gomimon prints 4000 DP + the seeded EX10-024 Kabemon's 2000 link DP |
| `BT23-009.test.ts` link replacement | `baseDp + 6000` | `10000` | BT23-009 Coachmon prints 4000 DP + the replacement BT23-007 Musclemon's 2000 link DP + the 4000 DP Coachmon's own link trigger grants |

The BT23-009 case is the clearest: the *engine* value never changed (10000 both before and
after). Only the captured `baseDp` moved, from 4000 to 6000, because the seeded link now counts —
so `baseDp + 6000` double-counted the link. The capture was replaced with the absolute value and
its derivation.

#### 3. Seam 9 ruling — `irKindTier2Cluster.test.ts`

`Tier-2 A3 — SetBaseDP (BT3-014 ...) REVERT-CONFIRM-RED` asserted `canActivate(ctx) === false`
for a board holding only an ineligible Lv.5 opponent Digimon.

Seam 9 changed that ruling (see `SEAM-9-21-MECHANISM.md`, "Ruling implemented", CR §15-4-2): a
**mandatory** triggered effect whose only action currently has no legal board target still
triggers, takes its place in the ordered set, and fizzles at resolution. So activation is now
`true`.

The test now asserts the ruling rather than the old gate:

- `canActivate(ctx)` is `true` — a mandatory trigger activates even with no legal target;
- no `setBaseDP` call is recorded — it resolves as a no-op;
- the ineligible Lv.5 Digimon keeps its printed 11000 DP.

The rest of the file is untouched.

#### Gates

Files changed:

- `packages/shared/src/effects/effects.json`
- `apps/api/src/cards/BT22/BT22-009.test.ts`
- `apps/api/src/cards/BT23/BT23-009.test.ts`
- `apps/api/src/engine/appFusionLinkPlacement.test.ts`
- `apps/api/src/engine/cards/irKindTier2Cluster.test.ts`

All 11 affected files, after the fixes:

```
 Test Files  11 passed (11)
      Tests  1003 passed (1003)
```

`pnpm exec oxlint <changed files>` — exit 0.
`pnpm exec oxfmt --check <changed files>` — "All matched files use the correct format."

`pnpm typecheck` does **not** pass on this tree, but for reasons outside this work: 36 errors,
all of them in `src/cards/BT6/BT6-017,019,072` and `src/cards/BT7/BT7-063,066,078` test files
being edited concurrently by sibling agents. None of the five files changed here appears in the
error list. Earlier attempts also hit transient `TS6053: File 'src/cards/EX3/zzz-debug.test.ts'
not found` / `src/cards/EX12/zzdbg.test.ts` from siblings' scratch files.

### P-107 Defense Training — ＜Delay＞ cost-reduction fix

Source: `docs/audits/EX10-reaudit/P-107-FIX.md`.

Out-of-set defect found while re-auditing EX10-032 (Q5092). See `REVIEW-NOTES.md`,
"Out-of-set defects found in round 2".

#### Printed text

```
[Main] Reveal the top 2 cards of your deck. Add 1 black card among them to your hand. Place
the rest at the bottom of your deck in any order. Then, place this card into your battle area.
[Main] ＜Delay＞
・1 of your Digimon may digivolve into a black Digimon card in your hand for its digivolution
cost. When it would digivolve by this effect, reduce the cost by 2.
```

#### Mechanism of the defect

`apps/api/src/cards/P/P-107.ts` modelled "reduce the cost by 2" as a sibling
`Replacement { event: "wouldDigivolve", mode: "reduceCost", amount: 2 }` listed **after** the
`Digivolve` action in the same `actions` array. Consequences:

1. The replacement was installed only once the digivolve it was meant to modify had already
   been paid for, so the ＜Delay＞ digivolution paid its full printed cost.
2. The replacement stayed armed after the Option was trashed as the ＜Delay＞ cost, so the −2
   fell on the **next** digivolution instead — the Q5092 breach (P-107's clause and another
   card's digivolving effect must not combine).

`packages/shared/src/effects/ir/actions/digivolve.ts` states the rule directly on
`reduceCostScaling`: a reduction belonging to a digivolve verb "must NOT be modelled as a
separate `wouldDigivolve` replacement — a replacement installed alongside the action cannot
reach the action's own digivolve".

A second, smaller defect: `into` was written as `{ filter: { … colors: ["Black"] } }`. `Filter`
has no `filter` member, so the black-Digimon restriction was silently dropped.

#### Fix

`apps/api/src/cards/P/P-107.ts`, second `[Main]` effect — follow the established pattern of the
sibling Training Option `P-108`:

- the `Replacement` action is deleted and the reduction folded into the verb as
  `reduceCost: 2`, which `runDigivolve` forwards as `costDelta: -2` for that digivolution only;
- `into` is flattened to the plain `Filter` shape used by P-106/P-108, restoring the
  black-Digimon restriction;
- `allowNoTarget: true` keeps the printed "may digivolve" branch able to end cleanly.

No engine change was needed. The "not the turn it entered play" gate is already intrinsic:
`registration/module.ts` gives every ＜Delay＞ `[Main]` clause
`self.enterFieldTurnCount !== ctx.game.state.turnCount`. The reviewer's note that P-107 lacked
that gate was a misreading of the IR; the new same-turn test proves the gate holds.

#### Red → green

| Test | File:line | Was | Now |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Q5092 P-107's ＜Delay＞, played and activated publicly, reaches this card only by the ORDINARY route` | `apps/api/src/cards/EX10/EX10-032.test.ts:1246` | `it.fails` | `it`, green |
| `Q5092 leak: P-107's spent -2 must not reduce this card's [Hand] [Main] cost` | `apps/api/src/cards/EX10/EX10-032.test.ts:1422` | `it.fails` | `it`, green |
| `OnDeclaration <Delay> reduces its OWN digivolution cost by 2 and installs no replacement` | `apps/api/src/cards/P/P-107.test.ts:424` | asserted the leaked `subscribeReplacement` | asserts `costDelta: -2` on `digivolveFromInstance` and zero replacements |
| `＜Delay＞ cannot be activated on the turn the Option enters play` | `apps/api/src/cards/P/P-107.test.ts:567` | — | new |
| `＜Delay＞ digivolves on a later turn for its cost reduced by 2, and the reduction does not leak` | `apps/api/src/cards/P/P-107.test.ts:596` | — | new |

The two new P-107 proofs run entirely on public intents: `playCard` places the Option,
`activateEffect` fires the ＜Delay＞ a turn later (BT3-067 Tankmon → BT10-064 Gogmamon, printed
cost 3, memory 5 → 4), and a following ordinary `digivolve` of an identical pair pays the full
3 (memory 5 → 2), so nothing survives the trashed Option.

#### Adjusted neighbouring test

`Q5092 negative: the activated ＜Delay＞ cannot put this card onto a Lv.3 [Sunarizamon] …`
(`EX10-032.test.ts:1329`) previously activated the ＜Delay＞ on a board holding only a Lv.3
[Sunarizamon]. That activation was possible only because of the defect: the trailing
`Replacement` action made the clause pass `canActivateEffect`. With the reduction folded into
the verb, the clause's single action is the `Digivolve`, and `canAttemptDigivolve` finds no
legal destination, so the engine offers nothing. The test now asserts that refusal — a stronger
form of the same negative claim — and then shows the `[Hand] [Main]` clause resolving on its
own at its own cost with P-107 sitting unused in play.

The `Q5092 leak` test needed a ＜Delay＞ that really resolves, so its board gained BT3-067 and
its hand BT10-064 as the ＜Delay＞'s legal digivolution. Its final memory reading is 2, not the
1 the red predicted: the `[Close]` Tamer suspends for 1 memory when the `[When Digivolving]`
clause trashes the `[Mineral]` card. The payment itself is asserted on the `memoryChanged`
event with `reason: "digivolve"` (3 → 1, cost 2 — no leak).

#### Gates

- `pnpm --filter @aegis/api exec vitest run src/cards/P/P-107.test.ts src/cards/EX10/EX10-032.test.ts --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism` — 34 passed
- `pnpm typecheck` — clean
- `pnpm exec oxlint` / `pnpm exec oxfmt --check` on the changed files — clean

#### Known neighbour, not fixed here

`apps/api/src/cards/P/P-106.ts` (Attack Training, green) carries the identical trailing
`wouldDigivolve reduceCost` replacement and the same leak. It is outside this lane's allowed
edits; it needs the same `reduceCost: 2` fold.

### Coordinator review notes

2026-09-08). Headings are demoted; the decisions and seam queue are unchanged.


##### Session 1 (2026-09-08)

Scope: independent re-audit of all 74 EX10 cards from base 46810fe1e. The 2026-09-05 revalidation claimed 74/74 at 10/10 with three narrative reports; none of that credit carries over. Priorities: the 17 suites with injected timing, the 6 suites with Digi-Egg security/deck fixtures, DigiXros cards (055, 056, 058, 059) and Tamer-source cards (062, 064), the ACE cards (010, 052?) and cards whose text depends on exact names versus substring names.

##### Engine seam queue

(none yet)

##### Catalog corrections

(none yet)

##### Once-per-turn on breeding-move watchers (decision, 2026-09-08)

EX10-004 lane: a `[Once Per Turn]` gate that hangs off `whenMovedFromBreeding` cannot fire twice in one turn through public intents, because the single legal breeding move empties the breeding area (`GameEngine.handleMoveFromBreeding` -> `breeding.actionTaken`). Decision (superseded in round 3): the EX10-004 round-3 lane found six implemented effects that move a Digimon out of breeding without the breeding action, and `movePermanentZone` fires `whenMovedFromBreeding` for effect moves; a second same-turn move is publicly reachable, so the gate is behaviourally provable and no ceiling applies.

##### Engine seam queue (session 1)

1. `immunity-grant-not-installed` (EX10-003 lane, retained red Q5009): `apps/api/src/engine/effects/interpreter/actions/grantStatic.ts`, `immuneToOpponentDigimonEffects` branch never installs the `beAffected` restriction. Reproduced on EX8-029, BT15-047, BT25-042 (whose security-trash cost is also never paid). Priority 1.
2. `attack-ended-still-enters-counter` (EX10-003 lane, Q5008): `CombatController.resolveAttack` awaits `runCounterWindow` before the `endRequested` check. Reported from code reading; a red test is blocked by seam 3.
3. `battle-area-counter-sources-empty` (EX10-003 lane): `GameEngine.counterEligibleSources` reports nothing for battle-area `[Counter]` cards (BT26-055, BT26-103, EX12-017, EX12-052, EX12-057, BT15-076, LM-001). Priority 2.
4. `granted-trigger-beaffected-gate` (EX10-008 lane, Q5014): `keywordGrantIsActive` re-checks `beAffected` on read, but no `beAffected` gate exists for a granted triggered effect in `subtriggers.ts` / `interpreter/actions/subTrigger.ts`. Endpoint currently matches the ruling; mechanism unverified. Investigate.
5. `attack-target-switched-source-convention` (EX10-002 and EX10-008 lanes): the generic "When attack targets change" watcher has no attacker-is-self gate; BT11-008 and EX9-054 gate it, EX12-019 and ST15-01 do not. Coordinator decision needed set-wide; no KB entry.

- EX10-006 `effectText` repeats "in its name" (EX10-006 lane). Same shape as EX10-008; fix both at closeout after checking the official English text.

##### Engine seam queue additions

6. `inherited-modifydp-duration-inert` (EX10-006 lane): `duration` on a continuous inherited `ModifyDP` has no observable effect (`"permanent"` vs `"forTheTurn"` identical across a turn boundary). No card of that shape can earn behavioural credit for its duration field. Low priority; document as rubric ceiling unless an engine lane finds a card whose printed duration differs from permanent.
7. `testkit-link-grant-affordance` (EX10-014 lane): no public intent or Advance Surface verb grants ＜Link +X＞; the Q5043 proof reaches past the seam with `(engine as ...).continuous.addLinkMaxGrant`. Precedent exists in BT23-014/BT23-021, but the ubiquitous language says a missing affordance is added to the seam, never worked around. Engine lane: add `advance(engine).verb.grantLinkMax(...)` (or equivalent) and flip EX10-014 (and BT23-014/021 if cheap). Coordinator scores such reach-throughs as Peer/stack 1.
8. `conferral-immunity-conformance` (EX10-010 lane): Q5022 and Q5025 need a card that confers an effect onto an opposing Digimon; that lives in the conferral ledger, outside any card-only lane. Engine lane: one conformance test for `GrantImmunity` plus conferral covering both rulings.
9. `simultaneous-when-attacking-ordering` (EX10-009 lane, retained red Q5656 against BT24-078): no `orderTriggers` decision for the simultaneous `[When Attacking]` pair (three plain optional prompts in engine order), and an already-queued `[When Attacking]` entry still resolves after its source stops being the top card. Seam: `apps/api/src/engine/effects/subtriggers.ts` and the `fireTiming` / `fireSubTriggers` queue in `EffectContext.ts`. BT24-078 still carries `@ts-nocheck`. Also the `[When Digivolving]` face: EX10-023 lane retained red Q5074 (two simultaneous When Digivolving effects resolve in registration order, no `orderTriggers` to the player). Priority 1 (production behaviour).
10. `breeding-phase-parks-with-stocked-egg-deck` (EX10-013 lane): a production Breeding phase parks with no pending decision whenever the Digi-Egg deck holds a card, so no turn-loop test can start from a stocked egg deck; Q5734 egg-deck bottom position is unprovable. Seam: `TurnStateMachine` Breeding phase / `testkit/advance.ts waitForMainPhase`. Check whether this is a harness gap (auto-skip hatch) or an engine gap.
11. `text-blob-excludes-requirements` (EX10-013 lane, latent): `matching/definition.ts:216` builds "in its text" from name, types, forms, attributes, effect and inherited text only; Q5038 also counts digivolution/DNA/DigiXros/Link requirements. No catalog card currently changes result. Low priority.

##### Fixture facts (session 1)

- BT1-010 and BT1-011 are not inert: both carry `[On Play]` effects (EX10-012 lane, verified in cards.json). BT1-012 has an inherited `[Your Turn]` +2000 DP when blocked. Truly inert: BT1-009, BT1-013, BT1-014. Brief updated. Suites that only seed these cards into deck/security/trash are unaffected; a suite that plays BT1-010/011 from hand must be re-checked at closeout (`rg -n 'playCard' ... BT1-01[01]`).
- `blocksCombatSuspend: true` on a `restriction: "suspend"` static is redundant: `ContinuousLedger.hasRestriction` already equates `suspend` and `beSuspended` (EX10-012 lane). Follow-up outside EX10: BT26-019, BT26-031, EX11-017.
- `youHaveNone` with no `zone` counts battle-area permanents only (`interpreter/scaling.ts`); breeding does not close the gate. No KB ruling either way; left as is.
- Suspend-count assertions are blind to once-per-turn gates: `Suspend` does not exclude already-suspended candidates, so a second firing that re-suspends the same Digimon looks identical to a refused use (EX10-016 lane, mutation-verified). Every EX10 OPT proof of a suspend effect must name the second target through `preferInstanceIds` or assert on a distinct candidate. Closeout sweep: `rg -n 'isSuspended\).length|filter\(.*isSuspended' apps/api/src/cards/EX10`.
12. `save-keyword-substring-overmatch` (EX10-018 lane, real defect fixed locally): `texts: ["Save"]` matches `＜Material Save＞` and `[Savemon]`, so BT10-111 and BT21-059 were legal cost-3 bases. EX10-018 now uses `texts: ["＜Save＞", "<Save>"]`. Repo-wide convention on BT12-008/011/035/048/058/061/075/076, EX10-015 and DigiXros materials. Durable fix: delimiter-anchored keyword tokens at `engine/cards/cardData.ts:499` and `engine/actions/digiXros.ts:465`. Engine lane priority 2; EX10-015 must be re-checked once the token rule lands.
13. `order-triggers-choice-evidence` (EX10-020 lane): Q5063/Q5736 need `autoOrderTriggers: false` plus a fixture that reaches two simultaneous turn-end effects; no engine defect observed. Evidence gap, low priority.
- Catalog: `cards.json` uses U+00A0 after bracketed names in 1488 effect/inherited/security fields repo-wide (65 EX10 fields). A scrape artifact, not an EX10 defect; `textContains` filters written with a plain space would miss those spans. Check at closeout whether `matching/definition.ts` normalizes whitespace; if not, this is an engine seam, not a catalog edit.
- Fixture gotcha (EX10-020 lane): a `[When Attacking]` effect that returns the only suspended opposing Digimon removes the battle target itself; seat a decoy and steer with `preferInstanceIds`.
14. `link-candidate-set-not-observable` (EX10-019 lane): the `Link` action raises no `selectCards`/`chooseTargets` decision, so a test cannot assert the offered candidate set; link filters are proved by outcome plus negatives. Low priority testkit/observe affordance.
- Reusable fixture fact (EX10-019 lane): BT21-101 Gaiamon prints ＜Link +1＞, so a two-link board can be built without reaching past the seam. Lanes 014, 016 and 017 could be flipped to it once the engine lane looks at seam 7; the coordinator may re-score those rows then.
15. `unaffectable-still-choosable` (EX10-021 lane, retained red Q5067): comprehensive 15-15-5-3 lets an unaffectable permanent be chosen; the engine filters them out of opponent-effect candidate lists at `interpreter/targeting/permanents.ts:138` and `:507` via `matching/permanent.ts:840` / `primitives.ts:5072`. Diverges for "choose 1, then X" effects where burning the effect on an immune target is a legal option. Engine-wide; ties to seam 8 (conferral) and the immunity seam 1. Priority 2.
- Fixture facts (EX10-024 lane): `deDigivolve` uses `levelFloor = 3`, so a Lv.3 top is never demoted (silent no-op); ＜De-Digivolve＞ proofs need a Lv.4+ top. An over-cap seeded link survives `setupEngine` but is trashed by the rule sweep at the next attack. BT26-086 prints ＜Link +6＞ (alternative to Gaiamon for multi-link boards).
16. `save-placeunder-position-default` (EX10-026 lane, real defect fixed locally): ＜Save＞ compiles a positionless `PlaceUnder`; `runPlaceUnder` reads `belowTop: action.position !== "bottom"`, so the saved card lands directly beneath the Tamer instead of at the bottom (comprehensive 4-3). EX10-026 now sets `position: "bottom"`. Same shape on BT10-020/021/075, BT11-015/077, BT12-008/086, BT14-057/059, EX4-016/018, EX10-015/027/039/044/045. Durable fix: default the Save keyword's PlaceUnder to bottom in the compiler or interpreter. Engine lane priority 1 (production behaviour, affects stack order and inherited effects); the EX10 lanes for 015, 027, 039, 044, 045 must add the same line or the engine default must land before closeout.
- EX10-015 (accepted 8/10) prints ＜Save＞ and predates seam 16; re-open with a bounded follow-up lane (add position bottom, order assertion) before closeout unless the engine default lands first.
17. `return-with-cost-skips-cost-without-target` (EX10-027 lane, Q5082): with no legal trash target the whole `Return` resolves as a no-op and the hand-card cost is never offered; the ruling says the player may pay and return nothing. Observable via trash-count effects (AD1-018). Interpreter-wide `Return` target resolution under `interpreter/actions/`. Priority 2.

##### Catalog correction applied (coordinator, 2026-09-08)

- EX10-030 Cometmon: the lower box was scraped into `inheritedEffectText`; it is the card's link effect (76 other link cards use `linkEffect`, EX10-030 was the only exception; KB Q5086/Q5089 call it "this card's link effect"). Moved to `linkEffect`, shared rebuilt. Module follow-up: the would-leave replacement must be a link-effect grant, not `isInherited: true`, with a stack-card negative (Cometmon as a digivolution card grants nothing).

All fire; not an EX10 defect. Candidate for a post-audit normalisation pass.

19. `any-digimon-digivolution-trash-cost-ignores-upTo` (EX10-033 lane, retained red, production behaviour): `interpreter/costs.ts` `payCost`, the "trash N from any of your Digimon's digivolution cards" branch reads `cost.target.count` only; `upTo` and `minimum` are ignored, so "up to 3" is a hard 3 (aborts with 1-2 candidates, never offers fewer with 4+). The `isSelfRef` branch handles `upTo` correctly. Priority 1; flip EX10-033's red when it lands.
- Fixture fact (EX10-034 lane): ＜Collision＞ grants no observable keyword; it is read at block legality (`combat/legality.ts hasCollision`). Probe it behaviourally (decline refused), never via `hasKeyword(x, "Blocker")`.
20. `security-shuffle-affordance` (EX10-035 lane, Q5108; also EX10-012 Q5034, EX10-020 Q5061): no public intent or Advance Surface verb shuffles the security stack, so "face-up cards turn face down on shuffle" is verified only by reading `primitives.ts shuffleSecurity`. Testkit affordance, low priority.
- Rules question (EX10-038 lane, Q5117): the link-card cost is trashed before the return target is chosen, so with an empty trash Copipemon returns itself. No KB ruling; left as passing with reasoning. Revisit if an official Q&A appears.
- Engine note (EX10-038 lane): `runAction.ts` preflights an empty target pool only for battle-area `Return`; trash-sourced returns skip it, so a return whose cost does not feed the trash can consume its cost for nothing. Related to seam 17.
- Rules question (EX10-036 lane): the costed `Unsuspend` preflight in `runAction.ts` refuses activation when the target is already unsuspended, so the player cannot pay the cost for its side effects (feeding ＜Fragment (3)＞). No KB ruling on activating a no-op-result effect; left as is.
- Fixture fact (EX10-036 lane): EX10-025, EX10-028 and EX10-032 all print an inherited "when effects trash this card from a [Mineral]/[Rock] Digimon's digivolution cards, delete" clause; they are not neutral cost-pool fixtures. Inert [Mineral]/[Rock] alternatives: BT10-062, BT10-064, BT4-065, BT2-064.
21. `pending-activation-lapses-when-source-leaves` (EX10-044 lane, Q5160; also EX10-058 Q5160): no general engine gate cancels a pending triggered effect whose source left its area before resolution; only per-card notes exist (BT20 AUDIT.md on Q4285). Affects every inherited-trigger card. Priority 2 (rules conformance, engine-wide).
- Harness facts (EX10-043 lane): `harness.ts buildPermanent` seeds link cards without `ledger.recomputeDP`, so a seeded `linkDp` card does not add DP until a real `linkCard` intent runs (DP baselines around seeded links are wrong); an over-cap seeded board cannot enter the turn loop because `trashExcessLinkCards` raises an unanswered `selectCards`. Testkit follow-ups for the engine lane (fold into seam 7).
- EX10-042 `effectText` repeats "in its name" (third occurrence in the set after 006 and 008). Closeout catalog pass.
- Engine wart (EX10-042 lane): `primitives.ts:2075` labels a default placement `addedDigivolutionCardsPosition: "top"` while the same call unshifts the card to the stack bottom. Not read by EX10-042; verify against any card that reads the field. Low priority.
- EX10-046 `effectText` typo "tash" for "trash". Closeout catalog pass.
22. `attack-stays-mid-resolution-after-collision-block-window` (EX10-045 lane, low priority): after an unblocked attack on the player while the defender holds a ＜Collision＞-eligible Digimon, `combat.isAttacking` stays true with no pending decision, so the next turn's `attack` is refused `wrong-phase`; `waitForMainPhase` does not wait on combat idle. Investigate whether the engine leaves an attack open or the testkit lacks a `waitForCombatIdle`. Reproduce from the EX10-045 report.
- Rules fact (EX10-045 lane): `[Once Per Turn]` with `sharedUseKey` is per permanent, not per card id; a second copy gets its own use. Correct per engine; shared-key proofs must use one permanent across two timings.

- Decision (EX10-049, Q5395): "add 2 to this effect's level maximum" re-reads the trash count after the mill (§15-6-2, effects resolve top to bottom with current information). Ratified; no ruling contradicts it.
- Testkit gap (EX10-049 lane): no public intent unsuspends an attacker mid-turn; same-turn OPT refusal on an attack-driven inherited clause needs `advance.verb.unsuspend`. Fixture alternative: BT1-095 Brave Shield played from hand (used by the EX10-046 lane).
23. `reduce-cost-deletion-triggers-not-batched` (EX10-048 lane, retained red Q5131): the `reduceCost` replacement's `payCost` (`interpreter/actions/replacement.ts`, `kind: "deleteOwn"`) deletes outside the play's trigger batch, so the deleted Digimon's `[On Deletion]` never fires and no `orderTriggers` with the card's own `[On Play]` is raised. RESOLVED by the EX10-065 lane: does not reproduce through public intents (Tamer deleted mid-game or seeded in the trash raises no prompt and grants nothing; the EX10-048 scenario raises no orderTriggers). The EX10-048 observation came from its injected path. Closed, no engine change.
25. `whole-clause-cost-gate` (EX10-052 lane, retained red): `Action.cost` gates only its own action and `CardEffect` has no clause-level `cost`, so "by trashing 1 card in your hand, [opponent may delete]; if not deleted, Recovery" performs the Recovery for free with an empty hand. Needs a shared IR field (clause-level cost) plus interpreter support. Related to the hand-origin reducer gate from BT23. Priority 2.

The `optional` gate is read on the `Replacement` action, not the nested `Prevent`. Closeout sweep candidates: `rg -n 'abortOnDecline: true' apps/api/src/cards/EX10 | rg -i delete` and every EX10 `Prevent` replacement.
- Engine nit (EX10-053 lane): `PlaceUnder` inserts multiple selected cards one at a time at the bottom (reverse selection order) and never lets the controller order them. Low priority.
26. `digixros-numeric-count-ignores-costReduction` (EX10-055 lane, latent): `validateDigiXros` (`engine/actions/digiXros.ts`) uses `requirement.count` as the per-material reduction whenever `count` is numeric, ignoring `costReduction`. Correct today only because every numeric recipe has `count === costReduction`. Fix before any `[DigiXros -1] 2 cards` recipe lands. Priority 3.
27. `place-under-prompt-without-legal-pair` (EX10-056 lane, minor): `canAttemptPlaceUnder` returns true unconditionally for `targetIsPermanent`, so a "you may" prompt appears with no legal source/destination pair. Same family as seam 18. Low priority.
- KB-INDEX note: Q5166/Q5167 are attributed to both EX10-056 and EX10-059 in qa.json; they describe EX10-059's gain clause. Covered by the EX10-059 lane.
- Fixture fact (EX10-054 lane): no inert Tamer exists in the catalog; choose Tamers whose clauses cannot fire in the board at hand (BT12-094 is quiet as a ＜Save＞ target). Seam 11 scope confirmed: the text blob omits structural DNA/DigiXros/burst/App Fusion/Assembly requirements.
- Brief correction (EX10-063 lane, verified in scaling.ts:87-107): youHave/opponentHas do not count breeding by default; the BT23-era brief line was overstated. Brief updated.
- Convention note (EX10-059 lane): DigiXros material stacking order differs by source (hand materials land bottom in reversed declaration order; a battle-area material keeps declaration order). §7-2-2-7 leaves order to the player; neither wrong, but the paths disagree. Low priority.
- Closeout sweep: EX10-012.test.ts reaches the engine with `as unknown as { cardSourceOf }`; replace with `observe(engine).cardSource(...)` (EX10-061 lane). Fixture fact: a play whose cost drives memory past the opponent hands the turn over immediately, swallowing an end-of-turn window.
28. `testkit-scripted-optional-responder` (EX10-062 lane): `autoAcceptOptional`/`autoDeclineOptional` are all-or-nothing; flows with two optional prompts needing different answers hand-roll a responder from `s.decisions` + `respondDecision`. Add an Answer Queue style option to `setupEngine`. Testkit, low priority.

Two sources of truth for the same card fact; a `playCard`-based test grades the table, not the module. Engine lane: make the intent route read the compiled IR (or derive the table from it). Priority 2.
- Documentation drift (EX10-064 lane): `cost.position: "bottom"` is inert for a loose card placed under the source Tamer in `interpreter/costs.ts` (the loose-card branch never reads it; default is bottom). Behaviour correct; align the code or drop the field.
- EX10-066 `effectText` repeats "in its name" (fourth occurrence: 006, 008, 042, 066). Closeout catalog pass; the 066 test pins the current string and must be updated with it.
30. `text-keyword-matches-reminder-text` (EX10-067 lane, latent): `textHasKeyword` matches a keyword icon anywhere in printed text including parenthetical reminder notes, which CR §4-22-5 excludes. No EX10 fixture hits it. Low priority; fold into seam 12's delimiter-anchored token work.
- EX10-071 `effectText` repeats "in its name" twice and `securityEffectText` drops "Digimon with" (fifth card in the set with the duplication: 006, 008, 042, 066, 071). Closeout catalog pass against the official list.

A Board Spec with an over-cap link and no decision flags hangs on the excess-link prompt (Q6370).

A ＜Delay＞ whose payload is an optional Link raises two optional prompts. A seeded link card that matches the Link target filter silently supplies the material after a link-trash trigger; EX11-027 is a clean non-matching host/link pair.

##### Engine lane 1 outcome (2026-09-08)

See ENGINE-LANE-1.md. Fixed: seams 16, 19, 23, 12 (+30), 7 (parts 1-2). Seam 1 disproved (EX10-003 test lacked the card-index import; card follow-up dispatched). Design-only with proposed shape: 9, 15, 25, 21, 29; the EX10-009, EX10-021, EX10-023 reds stay retained and their rows keep the scores recorded. Seam 24 closed earlier. Production behaviour changed by this run: Save stack position (all Save cards), "up to N" digivolution-card trash costs, cost-deletion On Deletion now batched with On Play (new ordering prompt), keyword-in-text filters no longer match ＜Material Save＞/[Savemon].
- Fixture fact (round 2): Appmon-link cards (014, 017) cannot use BT21-101/BT26-086 as hosts (not Appmon; the rule sweep trashes the link); `advance(engine).verb.grantLinkMax` is the right affordance there.
- UX note (EX10-020 lane, round 2): an `orderTriggers` payload reports `triggerCardIds` as the permanent's current top card for a permanent-anchored delayed delete, so a client labelling rows by card id alone shows the same card twice; effect keys still disambiguate. Web follow-up, low priority.
- Fixture fact (round 2, EX10-063/028 lanes): ＜De-Digivolve＞ (`peelStackTops`) emits `whenDigimonTopTrashed`, not the digivolution-card discard events; to trash an opponent's digivolution card publicly use BT15-019 Crabmon (bottom card) or BT1-099 Hearts Attack.

##### Out-of-set defects found in round 2

- P-107 (EX10-032 lane): in `apps/api/src/cards/P/P-107.ts` the `wouldDigivolve reduceCost` replacement is listed after the `Digivolve` action, so it never reduces its own ＜Delay＞ digivolve and stays armed after the Option leaves play, reducing the next digivolution (the Q5092 breach). Its ＜Delay＞ also lacks the "not the turn it entered play" gate. Two `it.fails` retained in EX10-032. P-set follow-up.

##### Round 3 decisions (2026-09-08)

- Seam 5 settled: a generic "When attack targets change" watcher (no "this Digimon's") fires for any target switch on its printed turn; only cards printing "this Digimon's attack target" (BT11-008, EX9-054) carry the attacker-is-self gate. EX10-002 and EX10-008 keep their unscoped IR; the 008 lane adds the comparative peer case.
- Rubric ceiling policy: a `[Once Per Turn]` or turn gate that no sequence of public intents can exercise twice in one turn, because the rules themselves forbid the second occurrence (documented with the rule reference and a structural proof), does not withhold the Behaviour point. Applies only after a lane has searched the catalog for a second public trigger and recorded the search.
33. `once-per-turn-consumed-before-condition` (EX10-008 round 3, out-of-set peer BT11-008): a watcher's [Once Per Turn] budget is consumed by a switch that its `triggerAttackerIsSelf` condition then rejects, so a later qualifying switch pays nothing (frequency decremented before the per-action condition in `interpreter/actions/subTrigger.ts` / `runAction`). Misprices BT11-008/010/014. Priority 2; no EX10 card affected.

##### Session 2 (2026-09-08): out-of-scope seams closed

Engine lanes (one Opus agent each, mechanism docs alongside this file):

- Seam 9 (`simultaneous-when-attacking / when-digivolving ordering`) and seam 21 (`pending-activation-lapses-when-source-leaves`): SEAM-9-21-MECHANISM.md. A mandatory triggered effect with no legal board target now still triggers and enters `orderTriggers` (CR 15-4-2), fizzling at resolution; the `whenAttacking` watcher bus now fires inside the `OnUseAttack` window, so `identityAtFirstCollect` retires a stale entry. Deferred trigger queues get the residency gate; seam 21's own red is outstanding (no public-intent fixture reaches it yet). EX10-009 and EX10-023 reds flipped; BT24-078 lost its `@ts-nocheck`; BT3-014's A3 test now asserts the ruling.
- Seam 15 (`unaffectable-still-choosable`): SEAM-15-MECHANISM.md. New `Target.allowUnaffectableChoice` forces the `chooseTargets` prompt when the only candidate is immune. Set on BT1-070 (the chooser in the EX10-021 fixture). EX10-021 red flipped. 219 "choose 1, then ..." candidates listed, not edited.
- Seam 25 (`whole-clause-cost-gate`): SEAM-25-MECHANISM.md. New `CardEffect.cost`, paid once in `runEffect` before any action; `canActivateEffect` refuses an unpayable clause. EX10-052 re-authored, red flipped. 19 migration candidates listed, not edited.
- Seam 33 (`once-per-turn-consumed-before-condition`): SEAM-33-MECHANISM.md. A watcher whose action gates all reject (and that paid no activation cost) rolls back its provisional once-per-turn mark. BT11-008 public-intent proof added.
- Seam 32 (`settle-silent-timeout`): SEAM-32-MECHANISM.md. `settle(predicate)` throws when the predicate never holds; `drainMicrotasks(maxTicks)` is the explicit drain. The throw reddened 453 tests in 347 files, all repaired by test-only lanes (categories: disguised negative proof, wrong milestone, late milestone) except the category-4 list below. Recurring root causes: `combatResolved` only fires for Digimon-vs-Digimon battles (player attacks end with `securityChecked`); idle `pendingDecision` is `undefined`, never `null`; block windows are intents (`declineBlock`), not decisions; digivolving draws 1 before `[When Digivolving]`; De-Digivolve floors at level 3; tests that import no card index register only the modules they import.
- P-107 (Q5092): P-107-FIX.md. Trailing `wouldDigivolve reduceCost` Replacement folded into `Digivolve.reduceCost: 2` (P-108 pattern); the ＜Delay＞ turn gate was already intrinsic. EX10-032 reds flipped. P-106 has the same trailing-Replacement leak; not fixed.
- Catalog: persisted IR resynced for BT10/11/12/14/19/21/EX10/P (the ＜Save＞ `position: "bottom"` normaliser output is the persisted form; BT21-catalog-sync now compares the runtime record like BT10 does). Link DP baselines in BT22-009, BT23-009 and appFusionLinkPlacement corrected (engine was right after seam 7 part 2).

45. `trash-return-cost-no-namedCounts` (BT18-019): `costs.ts` trash-zone `return` never writes `ctx.namedCounts`, so `namedCount` scaling reads 0. Engine, priority 2.
46. `zero-dp-check-ignores-cannot-be-deleted` (BT18-086): `anyZeroDpDigimon` loops against a protected 0 DP Digimon until the engine declares a draw. Engine, priority 1.
47. `link-orfilters-ignored-for-selfref` (BT25-093, BT25-101; Q6441/Q6443): `candidateLooseInstancesIncludingReserved` (`targeting/loose.ts:238`) returns on `isSelfRef` before reading `orFilters`. Engine, priority 2.
48. `replacement-prevention-preempts-on-deletion` (EX3-013, Q2212): a leave-prevention Replacement intercepts before the gained `[On Deletion]` trigger is evaluated. Engine, priority 2.
49. `batched-suspend-fires-one-watcher` (EX3-038): `verb.suspend([a, b])` fires only one of two `whenEffectSuspends` watchers. Engine, priority 2.
50. `trait-token-exact-vs-contains` (EX4-058): `match: "trait"` against compound trait "Mysterious Bird"; should be `traitContains`. Card-authoring. Priority 3.
51. `whenSecurityRemoved-default-seat` (EX5-014): SubTrigger lacks `sourceFilter`, so it watches the controller's own security. Card-authoring. Priority 2.
52. `opponent-option-vanishes-in-recompute` (P-179): the opponent's Option leaves the board during the continuous recompute after a digivolve, so the trash-1-Option cost fails. Engine, unexplained. Priority 1.

##### Session 2 gate

```
vitest run src/cards src/engine --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism
  -> Test Files 21 failed | 10703 passed (10724); Tests 24 failed | 36517 passed (36541)
  -> logs/final-suite.log, logs/final-failures.txt; the 24 failures are exactly seams 34-52
pnpm typecheck -> exit 0; oxlint/oxfmt clean on changed files (GameEngine.ts:4 unused import pre-existing)
node tools/sync-effects-from-card-modules.mjs --check --set <BT1|BT20|BT21|BT24|EX10|P> -> synchronized
```

- Worker-state leak fixed in `engine/effects/capabilities.test.ts`: a synthetic module built under the real id BT20-045 evicted Examon from the module-level Blast DNA keyword set, so BT20-025/042/045 and blastDnaCounter failed whenever that file ran first. Synthetic modules in engine tests must not reuse real card ids; `registerBlastDigivolveFromEffects` is keyed by id and runs on every `irCardModule` call.
- Cross-file registration is the dominant flake source now that `settle` is strict: a card test that plays a card it never imports gets the default behaviour alone and the real module in the suite (P-076, BT2-078, BT25-049, BT14-041, BT17-035, ST9-13 this session). Convention: import every card module the test plays.

##### Session 2, round 2 (2026-09-08): seams 34-52 closed

Five Opus lanes; mechanism docs: LANE-CARD-AUTHORING-MECHANISMS.md (36, 37, 39, 40, 41, 44, 50, 51), SEAM-34-45-47-MECHANISM.md, SEAM-43-46-48-MECHANISM.md, SEAM-38-42-49-35-MECHANISM.md, SEAM-52-MECHANISM.md.

- Card-authoring fixes (36, 37, 39, 40, 41, 44, 50, 51): modules corrected, persisted IR resynced. Engine gap noted: a clause-level `CardEffect.cost` (seam 25) cannot be paid when the clause's only payload is `Digivolve`/`DnaDigivolve`/`PlaceUnder`/`MindLink`, because `canActivateEffect`'s intrinsic gate runs first; BT13-010 uses a leading `Return` instead.
- 34: `Unsuspend` keeps ready candidates when the next action chains on the same target (`ctx.nextActionChainsSameTarget`); covers BT1-095 and BT24-047.
- 45: trash-return costs now write `namedCounts`.
- 47: loose targets union `orFilters` with an `isSelfRef` primary. BT25-093 also needed a breeding scope on its link recipient.
- 43: the filed cause was wrong (transient candidates are forwarded); the combat deletion trigger lacked `deletedTopCardId`, and the matcher now resolves the source's own host. BT15-073 was a test defect (inherited clause tested on a top card).
- 46: rule-check predicates skip permanents under a `beDeleted` prohibition.
- 48 (Q2212): a permanent's own deletion clause fires before leave-prevention. Follow-up after an EX1-073 regression (Q6030/Q6037): the pre-prevention fire is scoped to watchers anchored on the endangered permanent (`SubTriggerSourceScope`), so third-party "when deleted" counters only see permanents that left.
- 38: `triggerFilter.superlative` evaluated against the board pool in the sub-trigger gate.
- 49: batched suspends fire each watcher whose anchor is in the batch.
- 42: not a stacking bug; the optional-play preflight now counts a `deleteOwn` cost's product as a trash candidate. Test also needed `preferTriggerKeys`.
- 35: no engine gap; the deck test never imported ST12-08. Blitz stays false because memory rests on the controller's side after Ciel.
- 52: harness gap; `buildPermanent` now seeds pure Options with `placedByEffect: true` (override with `placedByEffect: false` to test the §17-1-3-2-2 sweep).
- Fixture repairs from the fixes: BT17-073 attacked with BT17-064 (now deletes a sourceless target); BT17-064 and EX1-073 played cards they never imported.
- Open rules note (BT17-064): "when this Digimon attacks an opponent's Digimon with no digivolution cards" is evaluated at resolution, not at declaration; an inherited [When Attacking] that strips the last source first makes the delete fire. Needs a ruling before an engine change.

Gate:

```
vitest run src/cards src/engine --maxWorkers=1 --no-file-parallelism
  -> Test Files 10733 passed (10733); Tests 36554 passed (36554)   (logs/final-suite.log)
pnpm typecheck -> exit 0
sync --check on BT1 BT11 BT13 BT14 BT17 BT20 BT21 BT24 BT25 EX4 EX5 EX10 P -> synchronized
```
